Through asymptotic efficiency analysis, we prove that the MAR estimator, which leverages partially-observed units, is both valid and more efficient than the MCCAR estimator when MCCAR assumptions hold. This result provides formal justification for preferring MAR-based estimation in policy learning under both missing data settings. Our comprehensive experiments using synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets confirm that correctly specifying the missingness mechanism is crucial: misspecified estimators remain biased regardless of sample size, while our estimators achieve near-oracle performance when assumptions are satisfied. Our work provides practitioners with theoretically grounded, empirically validated tools for robust policy learning in the presence of missing treatment data.

中文摘要翻译: 策略学习方法越来越多地被用于在预算约束下指导治疗分配。大多数提出的方法假设治疗数据完整，然而实际应用中经常出现数据缺失，这可能导致估计偏差并产生次优策略。我们通过将平均处理效应(ATE)的有效估计方法扩展到策略值和条件平均处理效应(CATE)估计，填补了这一空白，适用于随机缺失(MAR)和完全条件随机缺失(MCCAR)治疗数据场景。通过渐近效率分析，我们证明了在MCCAR假设成立时，利用部分观测样本的MAR估计器既有效又比MCCAR估计器更高效。这一结果为在两种缺失数据设置下进行策略学习时优先使用基于MAR的估计提供了形式化依据。我们使用合成和半合成数据集进行的全面实验证实，正确指定缺失机制至关重要：错误指定的估计器无论样本量大小都存在偏差，而我们的估计器在假设满足时能达到接近最优的性能。我们的工作为实践者在存在治疗数据缺失的情况下进行稳健的策略学习提供了理论上有依据、经实验验证的工具。

</details>


### [117] [Dysco: Dynamic Subspace Boosting to Mitigate LoRA Interference in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14367)
*Haobo Zhang,Jiankun Wang,Suraj Rajendran,Weishen Pan,Lam Tsoi,Yong Chen,Fei Wang,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco)，一种解决联邦LoRA微调中数据-参数干扰问题的插件方法，通过动态分配客户端特定子空间并融合，显著降低干扰并提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦LoRA微调中异构客户端导致适配器聚合不稳定，现有方法将聚合视为参数平均，忽略了LoRA更新子空间与客户端激活之间的几何对齐问题（即数据-参数干扰）。

Method: Dysco：每轮客户端从本地表示计算激活不敏感子空间并传输基向量；服务器通过封闭解构建客户端特定合并子空间，最大化与其他客户端不敏感方向的兼容性；采用多轮子空间增强以应对表示漂移。

Result: 在合成任务上，训练损失相比基线最多降低9倍；在MIMIC-IV临床笔记分类任务（Llama-3.2-1B）上，提升所有五种FL算法最高4.3%，优于近期联邦LoRA方法，且仅增加0.9%墙钟开销。

Conclusion: 联邦LoRA聚合应视为参数平均与子空间分配的结合；Dysco通过动态子空间分配有效降低数据-参数干扰，理论分析和实验均证实其有效性。

Original Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large pre-trained models increasingly relies on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to reduce communication and computation, but heterogeneous clients can make adapter aggregation unstable. We identify the data-parameter interference as a geometric source of this instability. This interference is controlled by the alignment between LoRA update subspaces and client activations, suggesting that federated LoRA aggregation should be viewed not only as parameter averaging but also as subspace allocation. We propose Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco), a plug-in method that allocates client-specific LoRA subspaces in a federated and dynamic manner. In each round, clients compute activation-insensitive subspaces from local representations and transmit only the resulting bases; the server then constructs client-specific merged subspaces through a closed-form solution that maximizes compatibility with other clients' insensitive directions. To handle representation drift, Dysco performs multi-round subspace boosting to preserve past update directions while adapting to future representations. We provide a convergence analysis that embeds the data-parameter interference as an aggregation-error term in a standard federated optimization bound, and prove that Dysco's server-fixed merged subspaces yield a tighter upper bound on this error. Experiments on controlled synthetic federated tasks and on MIMIC-IV clinical-note classification with Llama-3.2-1B show that Dysco substantially reduces interference, reduces the final-round synthetic training loss by up to 9 times relative to baselines under the orthogonal-subspace partition the theory identifies, improves all five tested FL algorithms by up to 4.3% on MIMIC, outperforms recent federated LoRA methods, and adds only 0.9% wall-clock overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/illidanlab/Dysco.

中文摘要翻译: 大型预训练模型的联邦微调越来越依赖低秩适配（LoRA）来降低通信和计算开销，但异构客户端会导致适配器聚合不稳定。我们识别出数据-参数干扰是这种不稳定性的几何根源。该干扰受LoRA更新子空间与客户端激活之间对齐程度的控制，表明联邦LoRA聚合不仅应被视为参数平均，还应被视为子空间分配。我们提出动态子空间增强（Dysco），一种以联邦且动态的方式分配客户端特定LoRA子空间的即插即用方法。在每一轮中，客户端从本地表示计算激活不敏感子空间并仅传输所得基向量；服务器随后通过封闭解构建客户端特定的合并子空间，以最大化与其他客户端不敏感方向的兼容性。为处理表示漂移，Dysco执行多轮子空间增强，在保留过去更新方向的同时适应未来表示。我们提供收敛性分析，将数据-参数干扰作为聚合误差项嵌入标准联邦优化界中，并证明Dysco的服务器固定合并子空间在该误差上产生更紧的上界。在受控合成联邦任务和使用Llama-3.2-1B的MIMIC-IV临床笔记分类上的实验表明，Dysco显著降低干扰，在理论识别的正交子空间划分下，最终轮合成训练损失相比基线降低最多9倍，在MIMIC上提升所有五种测试的FL算法最高4.3%，优于近期联邦LoRA方法，且仅增加0.9%的墙钟开销。我们的代码位于https://github.com/illidanlab/Dysco。

</details>


### [118] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型服务中监督微调（SFT）与上下文学习（ICL）两种个性化方法之间的计算资源分配博弈，揭示了用户个性化选择导致的拥塞效应，以及平台最优策略选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 个性化能提升模型性能，但消耗稀缺计算资源，用户需在昂贵的SFT与轻量级ICL之间权衡，且其他用户的个性化选择会造成拥塞，进一步影响激励。平台如何提供多种个性化算法缺乏理论指导。

Method: 构建了一个可处理的大语言模型服务框架，刻画用户在统计表现与计算成本之间的权衡；通过理论分析推导均衡性质，并在GPT-2线性回归任务上验证理论预测，同时调研21个主要AI平台的文档以佐证结论。

Result: 1. ICL与SFT在不同机制下各自占优，由预训练覆盖范围和数据信噪比决定，但拥塞会翻转排序；2. 均衡资源消耗呈现显著非单调性：提高预训练精度降低拥塞，而更广的预训练覆盖和更难的任务有时会增加拥塞；3. 提供两种个性化方法从不损害平台最大利润。

Conclusion: 平台应同时提供SFT和ICL两种个性化方式，这不会损害利润且可能提升收益；拥塞效应会改变用户算法选择，平台需根据预训练覆盖和任务难度调整策略。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）已彻底改变了AI服务，但一个关键矛盾随之显现：个性化提升了模型性能，却消耗了用户必须共享的稀缺计算资源。用户何时应投资昂贵的监督微调（SFT）而非轻量级的上下文学习（ICL）？其他用户的个性化选择所造成的拥塞如何重塑这些激励？平台在提供多种个性化算法时应采取何种策略？我们开发了一个可处理的大语言模型服务框架，刻画了用户面临的统计-经济权衡。我们的分析产生了若干令人惊讶的洞见。首先，我们证明ICL和SFT在不同的机制下各自占优，这取决于预训练覆盖范围与数据信噪比之间的相互作用，但拥塞可能翻转这些排序。其次，均衡资源消耗表现出显著的非单调性：提高预训练精度会降低拥塞，而更广的预训练覆盖范围和更困难的任务有时会增加拥塞。第三，我们证明同时提供两种个性化方法从未损害平台的最大利润，尽管可能增加计算负载。在GPT-2上针对线性回归任务的实验验证了我们关于算法性能的理论预测。作为这些结果的补充，我们对21个主要AI平台文档的审查显示，同时提供SFT和ICL的平台比例从2021年的9.5%增加到2025年的71.4%，这与我们的平台设计启示一致。

</details>


### [119] [A Noise-Robust Elicit-to-Optimize Framework for Distortion Riskmetrics via Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14373)
*Yang Liu,Yuhao Liu,Yunran Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个噪声鲁棒的“启发-优化”框架，结合逆强化学习和强化学习，从智能体的含噪决策中推断其风险偏好，并在条件扭曲风险度量下优化策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以从智能体的含噪（随机、次优）行为中准确推断其风险偏好，同时缺乏一个统一的框架来同时实现风险偏好推断和风险敏感策略优化。

Method: 在启发侧，提出自适应贝叶斯逆强化学习方法，从含噪观测中推断潜在风险目标，并证明存在有限个区分性问题可识别扭曲风险度量，收敛速率为O(exp(-cm+O(√(m log m))))。在优化侧，提出无模型强化学习算法，通过量化网络估计完整条件成本分位数函数，扩展PPO算法以统一优化各类扭曲风险度量目标。

Result: 建立了有限区分性问题集的存在性理论，证明了算法在一般设定下的指数级收敛速率，并在复杂金融环境中通过实证研究验证了框架的启发准确性和优化有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效处理含噪观测下的风险偏好推断，并统一优化多种扭曲风险度量目标，在复杂金融环境中表现出良好的性能。

Original Abstract: We propose a noise-robust elicit-to-optimize framework that integrates inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and reinforcement learning (RL) for eliciting agents' risk preferences and optimizing policies under a broad class of risk objectives characterized by distortion riskmetrics. On the elicitation side, we propose an adaptive Bayesian IRL method that infers agents' latent risk objectives from their noisy observed decisions, explicitly allowing agents to take stochastic and suboptimal actions. We establish the existence of a finite set of distinguishing questions that identifies the preferred distortion riskmetric within the candidate class and prove that the convergence rate of the algorithm is of order $O(\exp(-cm+O(\sqrt{m\log m})))$ under general settings, where $c>0$ is a constant and $m$ denotes the number of algorithm iterations. On the optimization side, we develop a model-free RL algorithm for optimizing policies under conditional distortion riskmetrics. By representing the objective as an integral of the conditional cost quantile function with respect to the distortion function, the method unifies distortion-riskmetric objectives. We optimize diverse risk objectives by extending the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm with policy, value, and quantile neural networks, where the quantile network estimates the full conditional cost quantile function and enables numerical evaluation of general risk objectives. A comprehensive empirical study demonstrates the framework's elicitation accuracy and effectiveness in complex financial environments.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个噪声鲁棒的“启发-优化”框架，该框架整合了逆强化学习（IRL）和强化学习（RL），用于在由扭曲风险度量刻画的一类广泛风险目标下，启发智能体的风险偏好并优化策略。在启发侧，我们提出了一种自适应贝叶斯逆强化学习方法，从智能体的含噪观测决策中推断其潜在风险目标，明确允许智能体采取随机和次优的动作。我们证明了存在有限个区分性问题，能够在候选类别中识别出偏好的扭曲风险度量，并证明了在一般设定下该算法的收敛速率为O(exp(-cm+O(√(m log m))))，其中c>0为常数，m为算法迭代次数。在优化侧，我们开发了一种无模型的强化学习算法，用于在条件扭曲风险度量下优化策略。通过将目标函数表示为条件成本分位数函数关于扭曲函数的积分，该方法统一了扭曲风险度量目标。我们通过扩展近端策略优化（PPO）算法，引入策略网络、价值网络和分位数神经网络，来优化多样化的风险目标，其中分位数网络估计完整的条件成本分位数函数，并支持对一般风险目标进行数值评估。全面的实证研究展示了该框架在复杂金融环境中的启发准确性和有效性。

</details>


### [120] [Integration Matters: Rollout-Based Training for Constrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14398)
*Xiaoxuan Liang,Saeid Naderiparizi,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过在线轨迹展开进行微调的框架，将约束引导融入训练过程，以对齐扩散模型的训练与采样，在不牺牲采样质量的前提下提高约束满足度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有约束生成方法存在训练-采样分布不匹配（训练优化）或分布偏移与昂贵调参（采样修正）等问题，尤其对于少步采样场景缺乏有效对齐训练与采样的手段。

Method: 提出一个微调框架，通过在线展开（online rollout）获取约束引导，并利用固定的噪声调度对去噪过程进行数值积分以进行端到端可微训练，使模型暴露于去噪轨迹中的约束违反情况，从而将扩散学习与采样过程对齐。

Result: 在多个任务上的实验表明，该方法相比先前方法，在保持具有竞争力的采样质量的同时，提高了约束满足度。

Conclusion: 通过在线轨迹展开将约束引导融入训练过程，能有效缓解训练-采样不匹配问题，在少步采样场景下也能高效实现约束满足与采样质量的平衡。

Original Abstract: Constrained generative models aim to produce samples that satisfy complex feasibility constraints while remaining faithful to the data distribution. Existing constrained generation methods typically enforce constraints either through training-time optimization or sampling-time correction. Training-time optimization approaches optimize on states induced by the training distribution, which can differ substantially from those encountered during sampling. Sampling-time correction methods instead modify the sampling process at inference, introducing distribution shift and requiring expensive tuning, particularly for few-step sampling. We propose a fine-tuning framework that incorporates constraint guidance obtained through online rollout into the training process, which aligns training with sampling by differentiating through the fixed noise schedule used to numerically integrate the denoising process. This exposes the model to violations that arise along the denoising trajectory and aligns diffusion learning with the sampling process. Experiments across multiple tasks show that our method improves constraint satisfaction while maintaining competitive sampling quality compared to prior methods.

中文摘要翻译: 约束生成模型旨在生成满足复杂可行性约束且保持数据分布保真度的样本。现有的约束生成方法通常通过训练时优化或采样时修正来施加约束。训练时优化方法在训练分布诱导的状态上进行优化，这些状态可能与采样过程中遇到的状态存在显著差异。采样时修正方法则在推理阶段修改采样过程，引入分布偏移并需要昂贵的调参，尤其对于少步采样。我们提出一个微调框架，通过在线展开（online rollout）获取的约束引导融入训练过程，利用固定的噪声调度对去噪过程进行数值积分以实现可微训练，从而对齐训练与采样。该方法使模型暴露于去噪轨迹中出现的约束违反，并将扩散学习与采样过程对齐。跨多个任务的实验表明，相比先前方法，我们的方法在保持具有竞争力的采样质量的同时，提高了约束满足度。

</details>


### [121] [LATTICE: Graph Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Spatial Omics Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14410)
*Jagan Mohan Reddy Dwarampudi,Veena Kochat,Suresh Satpati,Kunal Rai,Tania Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LATTICE是一个基于图的自监督框架，用于空间多组学数据（转录组+表观基因组）的跨模态整合，通过统一潜在空间表示实现多模态对齐，在黑色素瘤队列中验证了稳定性和性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的空间组学分析通常使用单模态流程，无法有效整合转录组与表观基因组等多模态数据，导致信息利用不充分，亟需能够统一表示多种空间组学模态的方法。

Method: LATTICE构建空间邻域图，使用TransformerConv编码器，通过掩码重建、跨模态对齐和空间平滑性三个目标函数进行自监督训练，将Visium RNA、scMultiome RNA、scMultiome ATAC、空间ATAC和空间CUT&Tag五种模态整合到统一的潜在空间表示中。

Result: 在包含11个样本、54,912个点的黑色素瘤队列上，LATTICE表现出稳定的优化行为和可重复的嵌入结果。添加scMultiome RNA使ARI提升0.157、NMI提升0.143、空间连续性提升0.174；更多模态进一步提升空间连续性和多模态效用分数。

Conclusion: LATTICE是一个实用且经验验证的多模态空间组学整合框架，能够捕获超越转录组相似性的染色质和调控结构信息，同时指出未来需要更强的监督信号和更广泛的外部基准测试。

Original Abstract: Spatially resolved omics studies increasingly combine transcriptomic and epigenomic assays, yet downstream analysis is often still performed using single-modality pipelines. We present LATTICE (Latent Alignment of Tissue-level and Transcriptomic Information for Cross-modal Embedding), a graph-based self-supervised framework that learns spot-level representations from harmonized multimodal features. LATTICE integrates five aligned modality blocks per Visium spot: Visium RNA, scMultiome RNA, scMultiome ATAC, spatial ATAC, and spatial CUT\&Tag. These modalities capture spatial transcriptomic measurements, single-cell inferred regulatory activity, and in situ chromatin and histone states within a unified lattice representation. LATTICE constructs a spatial neighborhood graph and trains a TransformerConv encoder using masked reconstruction, cross-modal alignment, and spatial smoothness objectives. On a private 11-sample melanoma cohort from an anonymized clinical collaborator comprising 54{,}912 total spots, LATTICE demonstrated stable optimization behavior, reproducible embeddings across analysis seeds, and complete multimodal integration across all samples. Adding scMultiome RNA to Visium RNA alone substantially improved concordance with Space Ranger clusters across 11 runs (adjusted Rand index [ARI] +0.157, normalized mutual information [NMI] +0.143, and spatial contiguity +0.174). Additional modalities further improved spatial contiguity and multimodal utility score (MUS), although they sometimes reduced agreement with RNA-derived reference labels, likely because the learned embeddings captured chromatin and regulatory structure beyond transcriptomic similarity alone. These results position LATTICE as a practical and empirically grounded framework for multimodal spatial omics integration, while also highlighting the need for stronger supervision and broader external benchmarking.

中文摘要翻译: 空间解析组学研究日益将转录组和表观基因组检测相结合，然而下游分析通常仍使用单模态流程。我们提出LATTICE（组织水平与转录组信息的潜在对齐用于跨模态嵌入），这是一个基于图的自监督框架，可从协调的多模态特征中学习点级别的表示。LATTICE为每个Visium点整合五个对齐的模态模块：Visium RNA、scMultiome RNA、scMultiome ATAC、空间ATAC和空间CUT&Tag。这些模态在统一的潜在空间表示中捕获空间转录组测量、单细胞推断的调控活性以及原位染色质和组蛋白状态。LATTICE构建空间邻域图，并使用TransformerConv编码器，通过掩码重建、跨模态对齐和空间平滑性目标进行训练。在来自匿名临床合作者的私人11样本黑色素瘤队列（共54,912个点）上，LATTICE表现出稳定的优化行为、跨分析种子可重复的嵌入结果，以及所有样本的完全多模态整合。将scMultiome RNA添加到Visium RNA中，显著提高了与Space Ranger聚类的一致性（11次运行中调整兰德指数[ARI] +0.157，归一化互信息[NMI] +0.143，空间连续性 +0.174）。额外的模态进一步改善了空间连续性和多模态效用分数（MUS），尽管有时会降低与RNA衍生参考标签的一致性，这可能是因为学习到的嵌入捕获了超越转录组相似性的染色质和调控结构。这些结果使LATTICE成为多模态空间组学整合的一个实用且经验验证的框架，同时也凸显了需要更强的监督信号和更广泛的外部基准测试。

</details>


### [122] [Adaptive Ad Load Design for Sponsored Search Markets: Evidence, Theory, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14418)
*Mohammad Rashid,Hema Yoganarasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模随机现场实验研究了安卓应用商店中广告加载量（1-6个赞助位）对收入、用户转化和参与度的影响，发现增加广告位可提升收入最多43%，但会降低搜索转化（最多5%）和日活跃度（最多2.2%），且效果因查询类型而异。在此基础上提出了一种探索增强的局部自适应广告加载算法（e-LAAL），在平台级部署中改善了收入-转化权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 赞助搜索中广告加载量的设计是核心供给端决策：增加赞助位可提高收入，但可能挤占自然搜索结果，损害用户体验。现有研究缺乏大规模随机实验对此权衡进行量化分析，且缺乏能动态适应查询异质性的自适应算法。

Method: 首先在安卓应用商店进行大规模随机现场实验（500万+用户，1-6个赞助位），揭示广告加载量对收入、转化和参与度的影响及异质性。然后设计e-LAAL算法：结合无模型的查询级决策规则LAAL（利用近期结果更新推荐）与静态探索臂（维持支持并提供固定策略反事实基准），并提供有限时间动态遗憾保证。

Result: 增加广告加载量最多提升收入43%，但降低搜索转化最多5%和日活跃度最多2.2%。高广告转化查询的额外广告位带来大量收入增益，低转化查询则边际收入很小甚至为负。e-LAAL在2230万用户和7760万次搜索的平台级部署中，相比静态基准、均匀基准和历史查询依赖静态基准，改善了经验性收入-转化权衡。

Conclusion: 广告加载量存在显著的查询异质性权衡效应，e-LAAL算法能够自适应地优化这一权衡，在实际部署中优于多种静态基准方法。

Original Abstract: Ad-load design is a central supply-side decision in sponsored search: more sponsored slots can raise revenue, but may crowd out organic results and degrade user outcomes. We study this trade-off using a large-scale randomized field experiment on an Android app store, where over five million users are exposed to one through six sponsored slots. Increasing ad load raises revenue by up to 43%, but reduces total search conversions by up to 5% and daily engagement by up to 2.2%. These average effects mask substantial heterogeneity: additional slots generate large revenue gains for high-ad-conversion queries, but little or negative marginal revenue for low-conversion queries. The trade-off also shifts within query as advertiser composition changes, such as brand-advertiser presence. Motivated by these findings, we design and deploy a novel adaptive algorithm -- exploration-augmented Locally Adaptive Ad Load (e-LAAL). e-LAAL combines LAAL, a model-free query-level decision rule that updates ad-load recommendations using recent outcomes, with static exploration arms that maintain support and provide fixed-policy counterfactual benchmarks. We provide a finite-time dynamic-regret guarantee for the e-LAAL architecture. In a platform-level production deployment serving 22.3 million users and 77.6 million searches, e-LAAL improves the empirical revenue--conversion trade-off relative to deployed static benchmarks and outperforms uniform and historical query-dependent static benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 广告加载量设计是赞助搜索中的核心供给端决策：增加赞助位可以提高收入，但可能挤占自然搜索结果并损害用户效果。我们通过在安卓应用商店上进行的大规模随机现场实验来研究这一权衡，实验中超过500万用户被暴露在1到6个赞助位下。增加广告加载量最多可使收入提高43%，但会降低搜索转化最多5%和日活跃度最多2.2%。这些平均效应掩盖了显著的异质性：对于高广告转化查询，额外的广告位带来了大量收入增益；而对于低转化查询，边际收入很小甚至为负。随着广告主构成（如品牌广告主的存在）的变化，同一查询内部的权衡也会发生改变。受这些发现启发，我们设计并部署了一种新颖的自适应算法——探索增强的局部自适应广告加载（e-LAAL）。e-LAAL将LAAL（一种无模型的查询级决策规则，利用近期结果更新广告加载量推荐）与静态探索臂（维持支持并提供固定策略的反事实基准）相结合。我们为e-LAAL架构提供了有限时间动态遗憾保证。在覆盖2230万用户和7760万次搜索的平台级生产部署中，e-LAAL相对于部署的静态基准改善了经验性的收入-转化权衡，并且优于均匀基准和历史查询依赖的静态基准。

</details>


### [123] [HyperShadow: A Benchmark for Detecting 3D Projections of Higher-Dimensional Spatial Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14419)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperShadow是首个公开的、将第4、5、6维度视为空间维度的基准数据集，任务是从3D点云中区分原生三维形状与高维空间（R^N, N=4-6）中刚体投影的“阴影”。实验表明该任务不同于内在维度估计，一个19万参数的点网络可达96.6%准确率，而零参数的刚性对齐残差（Kabsch算法）在时序轨迹上AUROC达0.982。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有“4D”数据集均将第四维视为时间维度，缺乏将更高维视为空间维度的基准。作者希望创建受控实验工具，研究哪些可观测统计量能证明数据与纯三维解释不兼容，从而探索高维空间投影的检测问题。

Method: 1) 构造高维刚体在R^N中的旋转及向3D空间的投影（阴影）数据集；2) 提出两种检测方法：a) 基于190k参数的点网络学习投影特征（密度折叠、径向剖面、拓扑变化）；b) 针对时序旋转轨迹，使用Kabsch算法计算帧间最优刚性3D对齐残差作为零参数刚性见证（rigidity witness）。

Result: 1) 标准内在维度估计器（TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE）仅达71-73%准确率；2) 190k参数点网络在四个损坏等级下达96.6%准确率，对未见物体家族泛化79-91%；3) 零参数刚性见证在时序轨迹上AUROC达0.982。

Conclusion: HyperShadow作为一个受控基准工具，证明高维投影阴影的检测需要超越内在维度估计的统计特征，点网络和刚性对齐残差均能有效区分原生3D形状与高维阴影。该基准为研究“数据是否与纯三维解释兼容”提供了标准化测试平台。

Original Abstract: Machine-learning datasets labelled "4D" universally denote three spatial dimensions plus time. We introduce HyperShadow, the first public benchmark in which the fourth, fifth, and sixth dimensions are spatial: the task is to decide whether a 3D point cloud is a native three-dimensional shape or the projection, the "shadow", of a rigid object living in R^N (N = 4-6). We show this task is fundamentally distinct from intrinsic-dimension estimation: a shadow is still at-most-3-dimensional data, and standard estimators (TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE) reach only 71-73% accuracy. Detection instead requires projection signatures, density folds, filled volumes with characteristic radial profiles, and topology changes, which a 190k-parameter point network recovers at 96.6% accuracy across four corruption tiers, generalizing at 79-91% to object families never seen in training. On a temporal track of rigidly rotating objects we introduce a zero-parameter rigidity witness: the residual of the optimal rigid 3D alignment (Kabsch) between consecutive frames, which must vanish for any rigid 3D motion but cannot vanish for the shadow of a rigid rotation in R^N. This single interpretable statistic separates the classes at AUROC 0.982. All data are generated reproducibly from seeds; the dataset, models, and code are released publicly. HyperShadow makes no claim about physical reality; it is a controlled instrument for studying which observable statistics can certify incompatibility with a purely three-dimensional explanation.

中文摘要翻译: 被标注为“4D”的机器学习数据集普遍将第四维视为时间维度。我们提出了HyperShadow，这是首个将第四、第五、第六维均视为空间维度的公开基准：其任务是从3D点云中判断一个形状是原生三维形状，还是生活在R^N（N=4-6）空间中刚体对象的投影，即“阴影”。我们证明该任务与内在维度估计有本质区别：阴影仍然是至多三维的数据，标准估计器（TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE）仅能达到71-73%的准确率。检测需要依赖投影特征、密度折叠、具有特征径向剖面的填充体积以及拓扑变化——一个19万参数的点网络在四个损坏等级下达到了96.6%的准确率，对训练中从未见过的物体家族泛化能力为79-91%。针对刚体旋转的时序轨迹，我们引入了一个零参数的刚性见证：帧间最优刚性3D对齐（Kabsch算法）的残差——该残差对任何刚性3D运动必然为零，但对R^N中刚性旋转的阴影则无法为零。这一单一可解释统计量实现了AUROC 0.982。所有数据均可由随机种子可复现地生成；数据集、模型和代码均已公开。HyperShadow不对物理现实做任何断言；它是一个受控的研究工具，用于研究哪些可观测统计量能够证明数据与纯三维解释不兼容。

</details>


### [124] [Active Real-World Factor-Based Evaluation for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14439)
*Andrew Liao,Hanchen Cui,Karthik Desingh,Aryan Deshwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种主动评估框架，将策略评估建模为序贯实验设计问题，通过概率代理模型自适应选择评估配置，以高效表征泛化机器人操作策略的性能并识别失败区域。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略的评估面临巨大挑战：真实世界性能受物体姿态、相机视角等多因素组合影响，穷举评估不可行；硬件评估缓慢且资源密集，当前窄测试集易遗漏关键失败模式，无法反映真实部署就绪度。

Method: 将策略评估视为序贯实验设计问题：在结构化的任务因素空间上拟合概率代理模型，自适应选择评估配置以最大化关于策略性能分布的信息增益，实现样本高效的性能表征和系统性故障区域识别。

Result: 在3个任务上进行了2331次真实世界评估，包含3种因素变化；相比典型随机测试，该方法通常节省至少20-40%的试验次数。

Conclusion: 主动评估框架能够显著减少评估所需试验次数，更高效地揭示策略在未见条件下的行为特征和失败模式，优于传统的随机测试方法。

Original Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies trained on large, diverse datasets have shown remarkable promise across a wide range of tasks. However, rigorously evaluating these policies remains a fundamental challenge. Real-world performance depends on a large combinatorial space of task factors including object poses and camera viewpoints, making full, exhaustive evaluation intractable. Additionally, real hardware evaluation is slow and resource-intensive, so current practice is to use narrow test suites that can miss critical failure modes and misrepresent true deployment readiness. We propose an active evaluation framework that addresses this challenge by treating policy evaluation as a sequential experimental design problem. Our approach fits a probabilistic surrogate model over a structured space of task factors and adaptively selects evaluation configurations to maximize information gain over the policy's performance distribution, allowing for sample-efficient characterization of policy behavior across unseen conditions and a systematic identification of failure-prone regions. We conduct 2331 real-world evaluations across 3 tasks with 3 factor variations and find that our approach typically saves the evaluator at least 20-40% of trials compared to typical random testing.

中文摘要翻译: 基于大规模多样化数据集训练的通用机器人操作策略在广泛任务中展现出显著潜力。然而，严格评估这些策略仍是一个根本性挑战。真实世界性能取决于包含物体姿态和相机视角在内的众多任务因素的组合空间，使得完全穷举评估不可行。此外，实际硬件评估缓慢且资源密集，因此当前实践采用狭窄的测试套件，这可能会遗漏关键失败模式并错误反映真实部署就绪度。我们提出一种主动评估框架来解决这一挑战，将策略评估视为序贯实验设计问题。我们的方法在结构化的任务因素空间上拟合概率代理模型，并自适应选择评估配置以最大化关于策略性能分布的信息增益，从而实现对策略在未见条件下行为的样本高效表征以及系统性识别易失败区域。我们在3个任务上进行了2331次真实世界评估，包含3种因素变化，发现我们的方法相比典型随机测试通常为评估者节省至少20-40%的试验次数。

</details>


### [125] [Depth-Dependent Hidden-State Collapse in Dynamical System Autoencoders for LiDAR Point-Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14463)
*Patricia Medina,Hy P. G. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于LiDAR点云分类的动力学系统自编码器（DSAE），发现当编码器深度达到 K=5 时，隐状态发生坍缩（标准差降至10⁻⁵量级），导致所有分类器均得到相同的宏F1分数0.224688。乘积系数特征增强无法改善性能或防止坍缩。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究DSAE在LiDAR点云分类任务中的表现，特别是编码器深度对隐表示质量的影响，以及乘积系数特征增强是否能提升分类性能。

Method: 使用不同深度（K=1~5）的DSAE架构分别训练，提取隐表示后使用随机森林、kNN和多数类基线分类器进行评估；同时引入乘积系数特征增强，比较xyz输入与xyz加乘积系数输入的效果。

Result: 在K=5时发生隐状态坍缩，隐状态标准差降至10⁻⁵量级，三个分类器均得到相同宏F1分数0.224688；证明了类间隐散度受总隐散度约束，而后者由隐状态方差控制；乘积系数特征无法改善坍缩前性能也无法阻止K=5时的坍缩。

Conclusion: 大深度表示坍缩是DSAE用于LiDAR分类的一个具体失效模式。

Original Abstract: We study Dynamical System Autoencoders (DSAE) for LiDAR point-cloud classification using spatial coordinates and Product Coefficient feature augmentations. The experiments compare separately trained DSAE architectures at encoder depths $K=1,\ldots,5$ and evaluate the resulting hidden representations with Random Forest, kNN, and a majority-class Dummy baseline. The main finding is a hidden-state collapse at $K=5$. For both xyz and xyz plus Product Coefficient inputs, the hidden-state standard deviation falls to the order of $10^{-5}$, while all three classifiers attain the same macro F1 score of $0.224688$. We prove that between-class hidden scatter is bounded by total hidden scatter, which in turn is controlled by the reported hidden-state variance. Thus a nearly constant hidden representation cannot retain substantial class-separating structure. Product Coefficients neither improve pre-collapse macro F1 nor prevent the $K=5$ collapse in the present DSAE setting. These results identify large-depth representation collapse as a concrete failure mode for DSAE LiDAR classification.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了用于LiDAR点云分类的动力学系统自编码器（DSAE），使用了空间坐标和乘积系数特征增强。实验比较了在编码器深度K=1至5下分别训练的DSAE架构，并使用随机森林、kNN和多数类基线分类器评估所得到的隐表示。主要发现是在K=5时出现隐状态坍缩。对于xyz输入以及xyz加乘积系数输入，隐状态标准差均降至10⁻⁵量级，同时三个分类器均达到相同的宏F1分数0.224688。我们证明了类间隐散度受总隐散度的约束，而总隐散度又由所报告的隐状态方差控制。因此，几乎恒定的隐表示无法保留有效的类别区分结构。在当前DSAE设置中，乘积系数既不能改善坍缩前的宏F1分数，也无法阻止K=5时的坍缩。这些结果表明，大深度表示坍缩是DSAE用于LiDAR分类的一个具体失效模式。

</details>


### [126] [Interleaved Noise Injection Improves Clean, Corrupted, and OOD Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14466)
*Matt L. Wiemann,Peter Melchior,Andrew K. Saydjari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种交错式（开-关-开-关...）噪声注入调度策略，替代传统的单调衰减噪声注入方式。理论分析表明，脉冲噪声近似雅可比正则化，高斯噪声起到曲率惩罚作用。交错式调度通过混合干净与噪声数据阶段，帮助优化器逃离局部最优并增强探索，同时引入梯度范数稳定技术来应对损失剧烈变化。在CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet-R上对ResNet和ViT架构的实验表明，该方法显著提升了模型对损坏、分布偏移的鲁棒性，且几乎零计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统噪声注入通常采用单调衰减调度，但交错式（交替开/关）噪声注入被发现在优化目标和鲁棒性上均表现更优。作者旨在从理论和实验两方面解释其有效性，并探索其正则化机制。

Method: 提出交错式噪声注入调度（在干净数据和噪声数据之间交替切换）；引入梯度范数稳定技术，根据干净梯度幅度缩放噪声更新；对脉冲噪声和高斯噪声分别进行理论分析，揭示其不同的正则化效果（脉冲噪声→雅可比正则化，高斯噪声→曲率惩罚）。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet-R上对ResNet和ViT架构进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法在损坏容忍度和鲁棒性上显著优于常见数据增强方法。将其堆叠在其他增强方法之上取得了最佳性能。通过显著图和注意力图分析揭示了不同噪声类型对不同架构的针对性正则化效果。

Conclusion: 交错式噪声注入是一种几乎零计算成本的有效工具，能同时提升模型在干净数据、噪声数据和分布外数据上的测试性能。脉冲噪声针对卷积架构的局部性偏差，高斯噪声减少注意力模型对大尺度虚假特征的依赖。

Original Abstract: Noise injection is a well-known technique in stochastic optimization. We report its surprising effectiveness with an interleaved (on-off-on-off...) rather than the usual monotonic decay schedule. We present a theoretical analysis of noise injection, which confirms that corruption by impulse noise approximates a Jacobian regularization, whereas Gaussian noise acts as a curvature penalty. This regularization behavior has been invoked to explain why noise injection increases model robustness. But the interleaved nature of our proposed schedule produces superior results even for the optimization objective: mixing phases of noisy data permits the optimizer to escape local minima and increase exploration without the risk of catastrophically forgetting the important features from the clean data. To stabilize this training scheme against the rapid changes of the loss when switching between clean and noisy data, we introduce a gradient-norm stabilization technique that scales noisy updates based on clean gradient magnitudes. We compare this method with other common augmentation methods and find substantial improvements in corruption tolerance and robustness to real-world distribution shifts on CIFAR-100-C, ImageNet-C, and ImageNet-R for ResNet and ViT architectures, with the best results being achieved by stacking our method on top of other augmentations. Through saliency and attention maps we show that the effect of interleaved noise injection stems from penalizing the failure modes encouraged by the inductive bias of the models: impulse noise works against the locality bias of convolutional (ResNet) architectures, and Gaussian noise reduces the tendency of attention-based models to pick up large-scale spurious features. Interleaved noise injection is therefore an effective tool to improve the test performance on clean, noisy, and out-of-distribution data at essentially zero computational cost.

中文摘要翻译: 噪声注入是随机优化中一项众所周知的技术。我们报告了其采用交错式（开-关-开-关...）而非通常的单调衰减调度时的惊人有效性。我们对噪声注入进行了理论分析，证实脉冲噪声的污染近似于雅可比正则化，而高斯噪声则起到曲率惩罚的作用。这种正则化行为曾被用来解释为何噪声注入能增加模型鲁棒性。但我们提出的交错式调度即使在优化目标上也产生了更优的结果：混合噪声数据的阶段使优化器能够逃离局部最优并增强探索，同时不会冒灾难性遗忘干净数据中重要特征的风险。为了稳定这种训练方案在干净数据与噪声数据切换时损失值的急剧变化，我们引入了一种梯度范数稳定技术，该技术根据干净梯度的幅度来缩放噪声更新。我们将该方法与其他常见的数据增强方法进行了比较，发现在CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet-R上，对于ResNet和ViT架构，该方法在损坏容忍度和对真实世界分布偏移的鲁棒性方面均有显著提升，将我们的方法堆叠在其他增强方法之上取得了最佳效果。通过显著图和注意力图，我们表明交错式噪声注入的效果源于惩罚由模型归纳偏差所鼓励的失败模式：脉冲噪声对抗卷积（ResNet）架构的局部性偏差，而高斯噪声则减少了基于注意力的模型倾向于捕捉大尺度虚假特征的倾向。因此，交错式噪声注入是一种有效的工具，可以在几乎零计算成本下提升模型在干净数据、噪声数据和分布外数据上的测试性能。

</details>


### [127] [Non-vacuous Generalization Bounds for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14506)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Rohan Alur,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为参数高效RLVR微调建立了非平凡泛化界，通过提出Progressive RLVR框架（结合RLVR、同策略蒸馏、TinyLoRA和模型量化），在保持84-97%性能的同时实现14,796倍可压缩性，并在数学、编程、常识推理和Text-to-SQL四个领域取得了比基础模型高9-51%、距微调模型仅6-11%的泛化界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）被广泛用于提升LLM的推理能力，但所得模型的泛化性尚不明确，现有研究缺乏针对参数高效RLVR微调在十亿参数规模下的泛化界分析。

Method: 1) 将PAC-Bayes压缩界适配到RLVR微调场景；2) 采用Gumbel-max重参数化技巧处理token生成中的随机性；3) 提出Progressive RLVR框架，整合RLVR、同策略蒸馏、TinyLoRA和模型量化。

Result: Progressive RLVR在四个领域（数学、编程、常识推理、Text-to-SQL）获得非平凡泛化界，泛化界超出基础模型精度9-51%，距微调模型精度仅6-11%，同时保持84-97%的标准LoRA微调性能，模型可压缩性提升14,796倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了参数高效RLVR微调在十亿参数规模下可以获得非平凡且紧致的泛化界，Progressive RLVR框架在保持高性能的同时显著提升了模型的可压缩性和泛化保证。

Original Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is widely used to improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), the generalizability of the resulting models remains poorly understood. In this work, we establish the first non-vacuous generalization bounds for parameter-efficient RLVR fine-tuning at the billion-parameter scale. Our approach adapts PAC-Bayes compression bounds to this setting, and addresses the inherent stochasticity of token generation by applying the Gumbel-max reparameterization trick. To operationalize these bounds, we propose the Progressive RLVR framework, which integrates RLVR with on-policy distillation, TinyLoRA, and model quantization. Progressive RLVR empirically retains 84-97% performance of standard LoRA fine-tuning while producing models that are 14,796x more compressible. We show that this framework yields non-vacuous generalization bounds in four domains: mathematical problem-solving, programming, general-knowledge reasoning, and Text-to-SQL. Our bounds exceed the accuracy of the base model by 9-51% and lie within 6-11% of the accuracy of the fine-tuned models.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）被广泛用于提升大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但所得模型的泛化性仍不明确。本文首次在十亿参数规模下建立了参数高效RLVR微调的非平凡泛化界。我们的方法将PAC-Bayes压缩界适配到该场景，并通过应用Gumbel-max重参数化技巧处理token生成中的固有随机性。为了将这些界付诸实践，我们提出了渐进式RLVR框架，该框架整合了RLVR、同策略蒸馏、TinyLoRA和模型量化。Progressive RLVR在经验上保留了标准LoRA微调84-97%的性能，同时生成了可压缩性高出14,796倍的模型。我们证明该框架在四个领域——数学问题求解、编程、常识推理和Text-to-SQL——均能获得非平凡泛化界。我们的泛化界比基础模型的精度高出9-51%，且与微调模型的精度差距仅在6-11%之内。

</details>


### [128] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高阶Runge-Kutta优化器（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2)对，带FSAL重用和局部误差步长控制）在严格计算匹配协议下性能不如plain Adam；其"自适应"本质上是假象（归一化误差远低于容差，步长始终卡在增长上限），实际等同于固定步长Adam加平均梯度，成本高出3-4倍。修复后在全批次场景下训练损失降低约40倍，但增益对初始步长敏感且无法提升测试准确率。后续实验排除了过拟合和显式温度控制等解释，确认梯度平均是真正的隐式正则化器，但RMSprop和NAdam以三分之一的每步成本就能达到或超越其效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将优化器解释为梯度流离散化激发了将高阶Runge-Kutta积分器应用于神经网络训练的兴趣，但现有文献缺乏严格的计算匹配评估，本文旨在严格检验其实际效用。

Method: 构建了一个具有代表性的Adam变体（基于Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对，采用FSAL重用和局部误差步长控制），在严格的计算匹配协议（每个方法获得相同的梯度评估预算）下进行评估，并与plain Adam对比。

Result: (1) RK变体在训练损失上输给plain Adam；(2) 自适应是假象：步长98-100%卡在增长上限，容差跨越100倍时轨迹比特级相同；(3) 该方法本质上是固定步长Adam加平均梯度，成本3-4倍；(4) 修复后全批次训练损失降低约40倍，但增益对初始步长敏感且不提升测试准确率；(5) 梯度平均是隐式正则化器，但RMSprop和NAdam以更低成本达到或超越其效果。

Conclusion: 高阶自适应积分能实现更深的确定性最小化和少量正则化效果，但没有什么是更便宜、调优良好的一阶基线所不能提供的。

Original Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

中文摘要翻译: 将优化器解释为梯度流离散化激发了将高阶龙格-库塔（RK）积分器应用于神经网络的兴趣。我们构建了一个具有代表性的Adam变体（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对，首次步骤最后一步重用（FSAL），局部误差步长控制），并在严格的计算匹配协议下对其进行评估，该协议赋予每个方法相同的梯度评估预算——这是该文献中很少强制执行的核算方式。在此协议下，RK变体在小批量训练和全批量训练（RK的最佳情况）中均在训练损失上输给了plain Adam。对其进行的检测表明，"自适应性"是虚幻的：归一化误差远低于容差，步长从第一步起就卡在其增长上限（98-100%的步长），并且没有任何相对容差×最大步长×初始步长的设置能使其真正发挥作用；跨越100倍的容差产生比特级相同的轨迹。该方法实际上是固定步长的Adam加上一个平均梯度，成本为3-4倍。修复它（真正的拒绝分支；对应用映射的误差）逆转了全批量训练的结果——比调优后的Adam训练损失低约40倍——而固定步长控制则将自适应性（一种涌现出的预热与增长调度）隔离为作用机制。但增益对初始步长敏感，且未达到测试准确率。一项预先注册的后续实验排除了明显的解释：更深的极小化不会过拟合，显式温度控制只会造成损害——留下了一个轨迹效应，即控制器选择的极小点在相等深度下的泛化能力比一阶下降低1.3-3.4个百分点。一项n=10的研究确认了一个次要效应：梯度平均是一个真正的隐式正则化器，在10/10的随机种子下击败了学习率匹配的Adam和AdamW——然而RMSprop和NAdam以三分之一的每步成本就能达到或超越其效果。高阶自适应积分能买来更深的确定性极小化和一个小的正则化效果，但没有什么是更便宜、调优良好的第一阶基线所不能提供的。

</details>


### [129] [A Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning Framework for Fine-Tuning Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14522)
*Zikun Zhang,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过随机控制方法，在连续时间框架下对离散状态空间的强化学习进行建模，提出了连续时间版本的PPO和GRPO算法，并应用于基于分数的离散扩散模型的微调，支持非可微奖励信号和中间奖励信号，显著降低了掩码扩散大语言模型的计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的方法仅依赖终端奖励，无法在去噪轨迹中引入中间奖励或优势信号；同时，离散扩散模型的强化学习微调缺乏连续时间视角下的统一理论框架，尤其是在掩码扩散模型中的策略参数化与概率比计算上存在效率瓶颈。

Method: 将强化学习建模为连续时间下的受控连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），推导连续时间策略梯度方法，得到CT-PPO和CT-GRPO；针对掩码扩散大语言模型提出轨迹子采样技术以高效估计轨迹似然，降低逐位置概率比的计算成本。

Result: 在低维熵正则化优化问题以及数学推理和编程任务的dLLM强化学习后训练中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的连续时间RL框架为离散扩散模型的奖励驱动优化提供了统一且高效的解决方案，支持非可微奖励和中间奖励信号，并显著降低了dLLM后训练的计算开销。

Original Abstract: We formulate reinforcement learning (RL) in continuous time with discrete state spaces and possibly arbitrary action spaces via a stochastic control approach, where the state dynamics are modeled as a controlled continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC). We consider policy optimization problems and derive the corresponding policy gradient methods, leading to continuous-time variants of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and group relative policy optimization (GRPO). As a primary application, we develop a complete continuous-time RL framework for fine-tuning score-based discrete diffusion models. The proposed framework enables reward-driven optimization without requiring differentiability on the reward signals. In contrast to the existing GRPO-based approaches that only rely on terminal rewards, our formulation allows intermediate reward or advantage signals to be incorporated throughout the denoising trajectory. Importantly, when specialized to masked diffusion models (MDMs), our framework encompasses a rich class of policy parameterizations over the vocabulary simplex with analytically tractable probability ratios, providing a unified perspective on exploration and policy optimization in MDMs. For masked diffusion large language models (dLLMs), we further propose trajectory subsampling techniques to efficiently estimate computationally prohibitive trajectory likelihoods, reducing the computational cost of computing per-position probability ratios. We showcase the effectiveness of our methods on both low-dimensional entropy-regularized optimization problems and RL post-training of dLLMs on mathematical reasoning and coding tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文通过随机控制方法，在连续时间框架下对具有离散状态空间和任意动作空间的强化学习（RL）进行建模，其中状态动态被建模为受控连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。我们考虑策略优化问题并推导相应的策略梯度方法，得到了连续时间版本的近端策略优化（PPO）和分组相对策略优化（GRPO）。作为主要应用，我们开发了一个完整的连续时间RL框架，用于微调基于分数的离散扩散模型。该框架能够在无需奖励信号可微的条件下实现奖励驱动优化。与现有仅依赖终端奖励的GRPO方法不同，我们的公式允许在整个去噪轨迹中融入中间奖励或优势信号。重要的是，当专门应用于掩码扩散模型（MDMs）时，我们的框架在词表单纯形上涵盖了丰富的策略参数化类别，并具有解析可计算的概率比，为MDMs中的探索与策略优化提供了统一视角。对于掩码扩散大语言模型（dLLMs），我们进一步提出轨迹子采样技术，以高效估计计算上难以处理的轨迹似然，降低了逐位置概率比的计算成本。我们在低维熵正则化优化问题以及dLLMs在数学推理和编程任务上的RL后训练中展示了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [130] [xHC: Expanded Hyper-Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14530)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Sunan Zou,Tuo Dai,Xiaoming Shi,Huaijin Wu,Yebin Yang,Zhuo Xia,Shaofeng Zhang,Lin Yao,Yuliang Liu,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出xHC（扩展超连接），首次实现超连接（HC）方法在N>4的有效扩展。xHC通过时间特征增强和稀疏残差流架构（N=16时仅更新k=4条流），解决了mHC在扩展时性能增益递减和训练成本激增的问题。在18B和28B MoE模型上，xHC相比mHC平均提升4.0分，且达到相同损失所需计算量仅为vanilla和mHC的1/1.50和1/1.19。同时提出xHC-Flash将每子层内存流量从73.5C降至40C，接近mHC(N=4)的34C水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Hyper-Connections（HC）方法（尤其是mHC）在N>4时性能增益递减且训练成本急剧上升，无法有效扩展至更大的N值。作者识别出两个瓶颈：一是流数量增加时回写信息不足，二是残差混合生成的计算量与N呈立方增长。因此需要一种能够在N>4时仍保持有效扩展且训练成本可控的HC方法。

Method: 提出xHC，包含两个核心创新：(1) 时间特征增强（temporal feature augmentation），为不断增加的流提供更丰富的回写信息；(2) 稀疏残差流架构，在N=16条流中仅更新k=4条流，同时保持对整个残差状态的高密度访问，从而打破计算量随N立方增长的瓶颈。此外提出xHC-Flash，通过优化内存流量使每子层内存流量从73.5C降至40C。

Result: 在18B和28B MoE模型上，xHC相比mHC在下游任务平均得分提升4.0分；达到相同损失时，vanilla和mHC所需计算量分别是xHC的1.50倍和1.19倍；xHC-Flash将每子层内存流量从73.5C降至40C，接近mHC(N=4)的34C水平，同时保留完整xHC的性能增益。

Conclusion: xHC首次实现了HC方法在N>4时的有意义扩展，使大规模N值下的残差流扩展在LLM预训练中既有效又实用。xHC和xHC-Flash共同解决了扩展瓶颈，为Transformer架构的残差流扩展提供了可行的规模化方案。

Original Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) expand the residual stream of Transformers into $N$ parallel streams, providing a form of memory scaling beyond model width and depth. Manifold-Constrained HC (mHC) stabilizes this formulation at scale. The large gains from $N{=}1$ to $N{=}4$ suggest residual-stream expansion as a promising scaling axis. However, existing HC-family methods typically stop at $N{=}4$. Our experiments reveal why: scaling mHC beyond this point yields diminishing performance gains and rapidly increasing training cost. We attribute this limitation to two bottlenecks: insufficient write-back information for an expanding number of streams and residual-mixing generation whose cost scales cubically with $N$. To address both bottlenecks, we propose xHC (Expanded Hyper-Connections), the first HC-family method to achieve meaningful expansion beyond $N{=}4$. xHC combines temporal feature augmentation for richer write-back with a sparse residual-stream architecture that updates only $k=4$ of the $N=16$ streams while retaining dense access to the full residual state. Across 18B and 28B MoE models, xHC delivers strong and consistent downstream improvements. On an 18B MoE model, xHC improves the average downstream score by 4.0 points over mHC, while adding only modest training FLOPs over the vanilla baseline. Scaling-law experiments show that the vanilla and mHC require $1.50\times$ and $1.19\times$ the compute of xHC, respectively, to reach the same loss. Practical large-$N$ training also requires controlling memory traffic from the expanded residual state. We therefore introduce xHC-Flash, which reduces the per-sublayer memory traffic from $73.5C$ to $40C$, comparable to the $34C$ required by mHC at $N{=}4$, while retaining the gains of full xHC. Together, xHC and xHC-Flash make large-$N$ residual-stream expansion effective and practical for LLM pre-training.

中文摘要翻译: 超连接（HC）将Transformer的残差流扩展为N条并行流，提供了一种超越模型宽度和深度的记忆缩放形式。流形约束HC（mHC）在大规模下稳定了该公式。从N=1到N=4的巨大收益表明残差流扩展是一个有前景的缩放轴。然而，现有的HC系列方法通常在N=4处停止。我们的实验揭示了原因：将mHC扩展到该点以上会导致性能增益递减和训练成本迅速增加。我们将这一限制归因于两个瓶颈：不断增加的流数量所需的回写信息不足，以及残差混合生成的计算成本随N立方增长。为了解决这两个瓶颈，我们提出了xHC（扩展超连接），这是首个在N>4时实现有意义扩展的HC系列方法。xHC结合了时间特征增强以实现更丰富的回写，以及一种稀疏残差流架构——该架构在N=16条流中仅更新k=4条流，同时保留对完整残差状态的高密度访问。在18B和28B MoE模型上，xHC提供了强大且一致的下游性能提升。在18B MoE模型上，xHC相比mHC将平均下游得分提升了4.0分，同时相比原始基线仅增加了适度的训练FLOPs。缩放定律实验表明，vanilla和mHC达到相同损失所需计算量分别是xHC的1.50倍和1.19倍。实际的大规模N训练还需要控制扩展残差状态带来的内存流量。因此，我们引入了xHC-Flash，它将每子层内存流量从73.5C降至40C，与mHC在N=4时所需的34C相当，同时保留了完整xHC的性能增益。xHC和xHC-Flash共同使大规模N值的残差流扩展在LLM预训练中变得有效且实用。

</details>


### [131] [Muse: Representation Geometry of Muon Beyond Normalized Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14536)
*Da Chang,Qiankun Shi,Lvgang Zhang,Di He,Yaoshuai Ma,Ganzhao Yuan,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Muon风格优化器中参数块表示选择对优化几何的影响，提出Method系列优化器，发现不同Frobenius等距表示会改变奇异值通道数、回拉缩放和非凸收敛常数，并通过实验验证了平衡的非原生表示可匹配原生表示性能，而缩减短边维度会使优化退化接近归一化动量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Muon风格优化器对矩阵动量应用极映射，但其更新依赖于正交化前每个参数块的表示方式。现有工作对这种表示选择作为优化器几何形式的研究尚不充分，因此作者希望系统研究不同表示方式对优化几何和收敛性能的影响。

Method: 提出Method系列优化器，在原生(native)、最近平方(nearest-square)、瘦(skinny)和向量(vector)四种表示下共享相同的动量规则和Newton-Schulz后端。利用Frobenius等距映射定义不同的极陡下降几何，并从理论上分析每种表示下的奇异通道数、回拉缩放和非凸收敛界常数。

Result: 在教师-学生模型中发现了曲率塌缩和各向同性Marchenko-Pastur谱分布，揭示了早期耗散与表示核范数-平方Frobenius范数比的关系。LLaMA2-130M和LLaMA2-600M的预训练实验表明，平衡的非原生表示可匹配原生表示性能，而缩减短边维度会削弱奇异通道支持和缩放，使优化行为趋近归一化动量。

Conclusion: 表示选择是Muon风格优化器的重要设计维度，短边维度决定了优化行为的质量；平衡的非原生表示可以作为原生表示的有效替代，而过度缩减短边维度会损失性能。

Original Abstract: Muon-style optimizers apply a polar map to matrix momentum, but their updates also depend on the representation of each parameter block before orthogonalization. We study this representation choice as a form of optimizer geometry and introduce {\method}, a family of Muon-style optimizers that shares the same momentum rule and Newton--Schulz backend across native, nearest-square, skinny, and vector representations. Each Frobenius-isometric representation induces a distinct polar steepest-descent geometry, in which the shorter matrix dimension determines the number of supported singular channels, the pullback scaling, and the constants in stochastic nonconvex convergence bounds. In a teacher--student model, curvature collapse and an isotropic Marchenko--Pastur spectral profile connect early-stage dissipation to the represented nuclear-to-squared-Frobenius norm ratio. Pretraining experiments on LLaMA2-130M and LLaMA2-600M, together with fixed-momentum diagnostics, show that balanced non-native representations can match the performance of the native representation, whereas reducing the shorter dimension weakens the scaling and singular-channel support, leading to behavior that increasingly resembles normalized momentum.

中文摘要翻译: Muon风格的优化器对矩阵动量应用极映射，但其更新也依赖于正交化前每个参数块的表示方式。我们将这种表示选择作为优化器几何的一种形式进行研究，并引入了\method——一系列Muon风格的优化器家族，它们在原生(native)、最近平方(nearest-square)、瘦(skinny)和向量(vector)表示之间共享相同的动量规则和Newton-Schulz后端。每种Frobenius等距表示都诱导出不同的极陡下降几何，其中较短的矩阵维度决定了支持的奇异通道数、回拉缩放以及随机非凸收敛界中的常数。在教师-学生模型中，曲率塌缩和各向同性的Marchenko-Pastur谱分布将早期耗散与表示的核范数与平方Frobenius范数之比联系起来。在LLaMA2-130M和LLaMA2-600M上的预训练实验，结合固定动量诊断，表明平衡的非原生表示可以匹配原生表示的性能，而缩短较短的维度则会削弱缩放和奇异通道支持，导致行为愈发接近归一化动量。

</details>


### [132] [CASP: Learning-Augmented Offline Approximation with Verifiable Certificates and Bounded-Loss PAC Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14545)
*Haifeng Li,Mo Hai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CASP（证书增强的解决方案剪枝）框架，在NP难离线优化中利用机器学习预测安全地忽略搜索空间的部分区域，通过多项式时间验证器检查预测的正确性，从而在不牺牲最坏情况保证的前提下加速优化。理论分析表明该设计具有可学习的证书参数和更优的泛化界，实验验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统上使用机器学习预测加速NP难优化时，要么要求预测器直接求解问题（这不可靠），要么无检查地采用预测结果会丧失最坏情况保证。研究者希望设计一种既能利用预测加速、又不会因预测错误而破坏正确性的方法。

Method: 提出CASP框架：使用机器学习预测建议搜索空间中哪些部分可以被忽略，但每个建议必须经过一个多项式时间的可靠验证器检查后才能被接受。该设计使诱导的损失类一致有界，从而证书参数可从少量样本中学习，同时保持最坏情况下的正确性保证。

Result: 理论结果：CASP的证书参数可从$\tilde O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log K)$个样本中学习，而未经验证的承诺类不存在分布自由的学习率，在成本扩散$R$下至少需要$\Omega(R/\varepsilon^2)$样本。实验表明，经过训练的预测器在分布偏移下，未经验证的剪枝会损失高达26%的最优解，而经过验证的部署不损失任何最优性。

Conclusion: CASP框架通过验证机制成功地将机器学习预测引入NP难优化问题，在获得加速收益的同时保留了正确性的最坏情况保证，理论和实验均证实了其优越性。

Original Abstract: Machine-learned predictions can speed up offline NP-hard optimization, but asking a predictor what to do amounts to asking it to solve the problem, and committing an unchecked prediction forfeits every worst-case guarantee. CASP (Certificate-Augmented Solution Pruning) instead asks which parts of the search space may be ignored, and accepts each answer only after a sound polynomial-time verifier has checked it, so correctness never depends on prediction quality. We develop the learning theory of this design. The verifier makes the induced loss class uniformly bounded, so certificate parameters are learnable from $\tilde O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log K)$ samples ($K$ the maximum instance size), whereas the unverified commitment class admits no distribution-free rate and, under cost spread $R$, none below $Ω(R/\varepsilon^2)$. Filtering noisy predictions by verifiable confidence dominates the standard min-combiner, with a margin we compute in closed form, and the prediction stays useful even given the LP, because it breaks ties on degenerate optimal faces, where every symmetric LP policy, meaning one whose commitments depend on the instance only through the verifiable confidence values, provably stalls. Experiments on five problems test the theory's quantitative predictions. With trained predictors, unverified pruning loses up to $26%$ of the optimum under distribution shift, while the verified deployment of the same predictions loses nothing.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习预测可以加速离线NP难优化问题，但要求预测器决定如何做等同于要求它求解该问题，而未经检查的预测会丧失所有最坏情况保证。CASP（证书增强的解决方案剪枝）转而询问搜索空间的哪些部分可以被忽略，并在每个答案经过一个可靠的多项式时间验证器检查后才予以接受，因此正确性从不依赖于预测质量。我们发展了该设计的学习理论。验证器使诱导的损失类一致有界，因此证书参数可从$\tilde O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log K)$个样本中学习（$K$为最大实例规模），而未经验证的承诺类不存在分布自由的学习率，在成本扩散$R$下至少需要$\Omega(R/\varepsilon^2)$个样本。通过可验证置信度过滤含噪预测优于标准的最小组合器，我们以闭合形式计算了其边际，并且即使在给定线性规划解的情况下预测仍然有用，因为它能在退化的最优面上打破平局——而任何对称的线性规划策略（即其承诺仅通过可验证置信度值依赖于实例的策略）都会在此停滞。五个问题的实验检验了该理论的定量预测。使用经过训练的预测器，未经验证的剪枝在分布偏移下会损失高达26%的最优解，而相同预测的经过验证的部署则不损失任何最优性。

</details>


### [133] [Probabilistic Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Estimating Heterogeneous Elastic Properties from Low-Resolution and Noisy Displacement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14563)
*Tatthapong Srikitrungruang,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络（PIE-PINN），用于从噪声、低分辨率位移测量中鲁棒估计杨氏模量和泊松比，通过B样条引导的位移网络和分层半柯西模型自适应加权，解决逆弹性问题的高度病态性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低分辨率位移测量下的空间异质弹性参数估计是严重病态的逆问题：低分辨率掩盖了区分异质性所需的空间细节，且微小测量扰动会被逆估计放大。现有逆方法依赖高保真观测和手动预设损失权重，适应性差且对噪声和分辨率退化敏感。

Method: PIE-PINN框架将位移观测、应变-差异和平衡残差统一建模为拉普拉斯分布概率模型。结合B样条引导的位移网络（B样条提供全局平滑表示，神经网络修正捕捉局部变化）和分层半柯西模型自适应降低严重位移拟合误差的权重。采用交替极大似然训练策略，通过加权残差最小化更新均值，并更新尺度以调整损失权重。

Result: 在不同噪声水平和观测分辨率下的系统性案例研究验证了PIE-PINN的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PIE-PINN通过概率建模和自适应加权机制，有效提升了从低质量位移数据中逆推弹性参数的鲁棒性，克服了传统方法对噪声和低分辨率的敏感性。

Original Abstract: Estimating spatially heterogeneous elastic properties from low-resolution displacement measurements is a severely ill-posed inverse elasticity problem because low resolution obscures spatial details needed to distinguish heterogeneous property variations, and small measurement perturbations or fitting errors are amplified through inverse estimation. Existing inverse methods often rely on high-fidelity observations and manually prespecified loss weights, limiting their adaptability and making them sensitive to noise and resolution degradation. We propose a Probabilistic Inverse Elasticity Physics-Informed Neural Network (PIE-PINN) framework for robust estimation of Young's modulus and Poisson's ratio from noisy, low-resolution displacement data. PIE-PINN models displacement observation, strain-discrepancy, and equilibrium residuals using Laplace distributions within a unified probabilistic model. To improve robustness, the framework combines a B-spline-guided displacement network with a hierarchical half-Cauchy model for displacement residual scales. The B-spline provides a smooth global representation of the displacement field, while the neural network correction captures local variations. The hierarchical scale model adaptively downweights severe displacement fitting errors, enabling more robust recovery of the latent mean displacement field. An alternating maximum-likelihood training strategy updates the mean through weighted residual minimization and updates the scales to adjust the loss weights. Systematic case studies across varying noise levels and observation resolutions demonstrate the robustness of PIE-PINN.

中文摘要翻译: 从低分辨率位移测量中估计空间异质弹性属性是一个严重病态的逆弹性问题，因为低分辨率掩盖了区分异质性属性变化所需的空间细节，并且微小的测量扰动或拟合误差会通过逆估计被放大。现有的逆方法通常依赖高保真观测和手动预设的损失权重，限制了其适应性，使其对噪声和分辨率退化敏感。我们提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络（PIE-PINN）框架，用于从含噪声、低分辨率的位移数据中鲁棒估计杨氏模量和泊松比。PIE-PINN将位移观测、应变-差异和平衡残差建模为统一概率模型中的拉普拉斯分布。为提高鲁棒性，该框架将B样条引导的位移网络与用于位移残差尺度的分层半柯西模型相结合。B样条提供位移场的平滑全局表示，而神经网络修正则捕捉局部变化。分层尺度模型自适应地降低严重位移拟合误差的权重，从而更鲁棒地恢复潜在的平均位移场。交替极大似然训练策略通过加权残差最小化更新均值，并更新尺度以调整损失权重。在不同噪声水平和观测分辨率下的系统性案例研究证明了PIE-PINN的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [134] [Gate-Zero Growth: A Geometric Framework for Function-Preserving Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14571)
*Dante Lok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零门增长（gate-zero growth）算子，通过零初始化门控添加残差块，在保持函数不变的同时实现可控的新功能引入，在Transformer扩增实验中遗忘率降低一个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在持续学习中扩增模型容量时，新参数的引入会不可避免地对旧任务造成功能漂移。需要一个既能安全增加容量、又能精确保持旧任务功能的算子，且该算子的几何性质需要被清晰刻画。

Method: 提出gate-zero growth算子：通过零初始化门控添加新的残差块，满足横截性条件后可诱导功能Jacobian的秩分离——旧方向不变、新权重方向梯度为零、新门控方向提供唯一的一阶功能变化。门控在持续学习中逐渐打开，功能漂移为$O(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|^2)$，Jacobian泄漏为$O(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|_\infty)$。

Result: 在300M→857M参数的Transformer（从WikiText-103迁移到BookCorpus）上，gate-zero growth在精确保持（Isolation）和联合前沿（Freeze-Nothing）两种操作点下均达到接近零的旧域遗忘（$\Delta_A < 0.1$），而非函数保持的对照组$G_{\text{stack}}$的遗忘高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: Gate-zero growth是持续学习中安全容量激活的规范实例，其局部几何特性涵盖了LoRA、ReZero和零初始化适配器结构，为这些方法提供了统一的几何解释。

Original Abstract: We introduce \emph{gate-zero growth}, a function-preserving (FP) operator for continual learning that adds new residual blocks through a zero-initialised gate. Under a transversality condition, gate-zero growth induces \emph{rank separation} in the functional Jacobian: old directions are unchanged, new-weight directions are exactly flat at the growth point, and new gate directions are the only first-order source of new functional variation. As gates open during continual learning, function drift is $O(\|\boldsymbolα\|^2)$ and Jacobian leakage $O(\|\boldsymbolα\|_\infty)$, giving a controlled departure from the FP locus. On a $300\mathrm{M}\to857\mathrm{M}$ Transformer adapted from WikiText-103 to BookCorpus, gate-zero growth reaches near-zero old-domain forgetting ($Δ_A < 0.1$) under both exact-preservation (Isolation) and joint-frontier (Freeze-Nothing) operating points, while a non-FP control ($G_{\text{stack}}$) suffers an order-of-magnitude larger forgetting under the same recipe. The same geometric analysis covers LoRA, ReZero, and zero-init adapter constructions, establishing gate-zero growth as the canonical instance of a shared local geometry that governs safe capacity activation in CL.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了\textbf{零门增长}（gate-zero growth）——一种用于持续学习的函数保持（FP）算子，通过零初始化门控添加新的残差块。在横截性条件下，零门增长在功能Jacobian中诱导出\textbf{秩分离}：旧方向保持不变，新权重方向在增长点处恰好平坦，而新门控方向是唯一的新功能变化的一阶来源。当门控在持续学习过程中逐渐打开时，功能漂移为$O(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|^2)$，Jacobian泄漏为$O(\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}\|_\infty)$，从而实现对函数保持轨迹的受控偏离。在从WikiText-103迁移到BookCorpus的300M→857M Transformer上，零门增长在精确保持（Isolation）和联合前沿（Freeze-Nothing）两种操作点下均达到接近零的旧域遗忘（$\Delta_A < 0.1$），而采用相同训练方案的非函数保持对照组（$G_{\text{stack}}$）的遗忘高出一个数量级。相同的几何分析涵盖了LoRA、ReZero和零初始化适配器结构，确立了零门增长作为持续学习中安全容量激活的共享局部几何结构的规范实例。

</details>


### [135] [Sharp Stability Threshold and Certification for Designing Stable Residual Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14576)
*Hyemin Gu,Michael Tyrrell,Tuhin Sahai,Markos A. Katsoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度残差架构的"次线性增长原则"：残差块速度场的输入幅度指数 q ≤ 1 是稳定训练的必要充分条件。通过常微分方程理论和最优控制分析双重论证建立 q=1 阈值，并给出基于基本运算的指数算术规则以高效认证架构稳定性。实验在Mamba和PatchTST上验证了该判据的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度残差网络的训练稳定性缺乏理论准则，现有方法依赖层归一化等启发式技巧。本文旨在建立严格的稳定性条件，解释归一化层的作用机理，并替代试错式的稳定架构搜索。

Method: 1) 提出残差块速度场的输入幅度指数 q 作为稳定性判据；2) 用经典ODE理论证明 q≤1 保证全局前向流，q>1 导致发散；3) 用HJB方程的最优控制分析证明训练最优解是边界上的bang-bang行为，q>1 时最优解爆破；4) 给出残差块的五种基本运算的指数算术规则，实现架构级的稳定性认证。

Result: 理论上建立 q≤1 作为稳定训练的必要充分条件；实验上将Mamba块从 q=5 降至 q=1（无需层归一化）并稳定训练；在Mamba和PatchTST上验证了 q≤1 变体的稳定训练，证明判据是输入幅度指数而非归一化层本身。

Conclusion: 次线性增长原则为深度残差架构提供了严格的稳定性理论，解释了层归一化的稳定化作用，并为稳定架构设计提供了可计算、无需试错的设计准则。

Original Abstract: We propose \emph{the sublinear-growth principle} for deep residual architectures -- a sharp stability threshold on the input-magnitude exponent of every residual block's velocity field: $$\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\,\|x\|^q + b, \qquad q \in [0, 1].$$ The threshold $q = 1$ is established via two independent arguments. Classical ODE theory gives a global forward flow on $[0, T]$ at $q \le 1$ and exhibits divergent velocity fields at any $q > 1$. The optimal-control analysis, via the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, sharpens this to a selection statement: the training optimum is bang-bang on the boundary of the admissible class, so the optimum at $q > 1$ blows up while the optimum at $q \le 1$ is safe by construction. The exponent criterion $q \le 1$ is thereby a necessary and sufficient condition for stable training. It clarifies architectural placements that ensure the stability of training and inference, explaining, for instance, the stabilizing role of layer normalization. The sublinear-growth velocity fields form \emph{the right function space} on which forward dynamics, adjoint sensitivity, and architectural composition are all well-controlled. An arithmetic of input-magnitude exponents under the five operations that build residual blocks enables efficient certification of $q_k \le 1$ at the level of architectural primitives, in place of ad hoc trial and error in the search for stable neural architectural designs. A parameter-free modification reduces the supercritical Mamba block from $q = 5$ to $q = 1$ without layer normalization, demonstrating this point. Experiments on Mamba and PatchTST confirm that the $q \le 1$ variants train stably: the criterion is the input-magnitude exponent, not the presence of a normalization layer.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了深度残差架构的\textbf{次线性增长原则}——每个残差块速度场输入幅度指数上的一个尖锐稳定性阈值：$$\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\,\|x\|^q + b, \qquad q \in [0, 1].$$ 阈值 $q=1$ 通过两个独立论证建立。经典常微分方程理论给出 $q \le 1$ 时在 $[0, T]$ 上的全局前向流，并表明任何 $q > 1$ 都会出现发散速度场。通过 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman 方程进行的最优控制分析将该结果精炼为一个选择论断：训练最优解在可容许类的边界上是 bang-bang 的，因此 $q > 1$ 时的最优解会爆破，而 $q \le 1$ 时的最优解通过构造是安全的。指数判据 $q \le 1$ 由此成为稳定训练的必要充分条件。它阐明了确保训练和推理稳定性的架构布局，例如解释了层归一化的稳定化作用。次线性增长速度场构成了\textbf{正确的函数空间}，在此空间上前向动力学、伴随敏感性和架构组合均得到良好控制。在构建残差块的五种运算下输入幅度指数的算术规则，使得能够在架构原语层面高效认证 $q_k \le 1$，从而替代在搜索稳定神经架构设计时的特定试错。一个无参数修改将超临界 Mamba 块从 $q = 5$ 降至 $q = 1$ 而无需层归一化，证明了这一要点。在 Mamba 和 PatchTST 上的实验证实，$q \le 1$ 变体训练稳定：判据是输入幅度指数，而非归一化层的存在。

</details>


### [136] [Accelerating A/B-Tests with Counterfactual Estimation: Reducing Variance through Policy Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14604)
*Olivier Jeunen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用策略重叠（policy overlap）来提升A/B测试效率的新实验协议，通过Δ-离策略估计方法在策略有共同支持域时获得比标准差异均值估计更低的方差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准A/B测试虽然是在线平台假设检验的黄金标准，但运行成本高且方差问题影响统计功效。现有方差缩减技术忽略了竞争策略之间潜在的结构关系——当处理和对照策略在某个动作上一致时，结果只贡献噪声而无信号，不必要地扩大了置信区间。

Method: 将随机化处理分配机制重新框架化为元策略（meta-policy），利用Δ-离策略估计（Δ-Off-Policy Estimation）方法获得平均处理效应的无偏估计。核心思想是识别并利用策略重叠区域，使得方差随策略间散度（divergence）而非原始结果方差缩放。

Result: 理论分析证明该方法在一般情况下恢复标准A/B测试实践，但当策略有共同支持域时，其方差严格优于标准差异均值估计量。当重叠区域贡献非零残差方差时，改进是严格的。实验结果验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 该方法有望对推荐系统、信息检索管道和大语言模型接口的实际评估产生显著影响，通过利用策略重叠加速在线实验。

Original Abstract: Online controlled experiments are the gold standard for hypothesis testing in online platforms. Notwithstanding their ubiquity, they are notoriously expensive to run, and issues of variance hamper statistical power in assessing treatment effects. While standard variance reduction techniques leverage model-based control variates to reduce outcome noise, they remain agnostic to potential structural relationships between competing policies.
  In this work, we identify a critical inefficiency in the standard A/B-testing protocol: when a treatment and control policy agree on an action, the resulting outcome contributes noise but no signal regarding the treatment effect -- unnecessarily inflating confidence intervals. We propose a novel experimental protocol that exploits this policy overlap to accelerate experimentation. The key insight is to frame the randomised treatment assignment mechanism as a meta-policy, and leverage $Δ$-Off-Policy Estimation methods to obtain unbiased estimates for average treatment effects. We prove analytically that our approach recovers standard A/B-testing practices in the general case, but that its variance scales with the divergence between policies rather than raw outcome variance. Hence, we dominate the standard Difference-in-Means estimator whenever policies have common support, and the improvement is strict whenever the overlap region contributes non-zero residual variance. Empirical results corroborate these theoretical insights -- holding promise for significant impact on the real-world evaluation of recommender systems, information retrieval pipelines, and large language model interfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 在线对照实验是在线平台中进行假设检验的黄金标准。尽管它们被广泛使用，但运行成本极高，且方差问题阻碍了评估处理效应时的统计功效。标准的方差缩减技术虽然利用基于模型的控制变量来降低结果噪声，但忽视了竞争策略之间潜在的结构关系。
  在这项工作中，我们识别出标准A/B测试协议中的一个关键低效问题：当处理策略和对照策略在某个动作上达成一致时，产生的结果只贡献噪声而不携带关于处理效应的信号——不必要地扩大了置信区间。我们提出了一种新的实验协议，利用这种策略重叠来加速实验。关键洞察是将随机化处理分配机制重新框架化为元策略，并利用Δ-离策略估计方法获得平均处理效应的无偏估计。我们从理论上证明，该方法在一般情况下可以恢复标准A/B测试实践，但其方差随策略间的散度而非原始结果方差缩放。因此，只要策略具有共同支持域，我们的方法就优于标准差异均值估计量；当重叠区域贡献非零残差方差时，改进是严格的。实验结果验证了这些理论洞见——对推荐系统、信息检索管道和大语言模型接口的实际评估有望产生显著影响。

</details>


### [137] [Auditing Fairness-Privacy Trade-offs: Subpopulation-Level Effects of Fairness-Enhancing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14607)
*Umid Suleymanov,Ilhama Novruzova,Khalid Mammadov,Natavan Hasanova,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了公平性增强算法对子群体级别成员推理隐私风险的影响，揭示了公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险，其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略，并提出了首个统一的实证审计框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先前工作主要研究隐私保护技术如何影响公平性，但反过来——公平性增强算法如何影响隐私泄露——这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将似然比攻击（LiRA）适配到子群体审计中，以发现聚合评估所掩盖的隐私差异；分析差分隐私（DP）与不同类别公平性增强方法的交互作用。

Result: 公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险；其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略；DP的隐私收益和效用成本在子群体间分布不均。

Conclusion: 公平性、隐私和效用必须在子群体层面进行联合评估，并提出了首个统一实证框架来支持这种审计实践。

Original Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, law enforcement, and finance must satisfy not only utility requirements but also fairness and privacy guarantees. While prior work has largely examined how privacy-preserving techniques affect fairness, the inverse question-how fairness-enhancing algorithms influence privacy leakage-remains underexplored. We present the first comprehensive study of how fairness interventions affect membership inference privacy risks at the subpopulation level. By adapting the Likelihood Ratio Attack (LiRA) for subgroup auditing, we uncover privacy disparities that aggregate evaluations obscure. We further analyze how Differential Privacy (DP) interacts with fairness-enhancing methods across different categories, showing that DP's privacy benefits and utility costs are unevenly distributed across subpopulations. Our results demonstrate that fairness interventions do not uniformly increase privacy risk; their impact depends on model architecture, subgroup size, and mitigation strategy. These findings reveal that fairness, privacy, and utility must be jointly evaluated at the subpopulation level, and we introduce the first unified empirical framework to support such auditing in practice.

中文摘要翻译: 部署在医疗、执法和金融等敏感领域的机器学习模型不仅需要满足效用要求，还必须兼顾公平性和隐私保障。虽然先前工作主要研究了隐私保护技术如何影响公平性，但反过来——公平性增强算法如何影响隐私泄露——这一问题仍未被充分探索。我们首次系统研究了公平性干预如何影响子群体级别的成员推理隐私风险。通过将似然比攻击（LiRA）适配到子群体审计中，我们发现了聚合评估所掩盖的隐私差异。我们进一步分析了差分隐私（DP）与不同类别公平性增强方法的交互作用，表明DP的隐私收益和效用成本在子群体间分布不均。我们的结果表明，公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险；其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略。这些发现表明，公平性、隐私和效用必须在子群体层面进行联合评估，我们提出了首个统一的实证框架来支持这种审计实践。

</details>


### [138] [Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning for Long-Tailed Electrocardiogram Arrhythmia Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14613)
*Jin Dai,Qiuzhen Zhang,Chenyun Dai,Danmei Lan,Can Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Angular Gaussian监督对比学习（AG-SCL）框架，通过全协方差不确定性建模、自适应对数调整和尾部感知增强，解决长尾心电图心律失常诊断中罕见类识别困难问题，在PTB-XL和Noc-ECG数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长尾标签分布降低了深度学习在心电图心律失常诊断中的可靠性，尤其是临床重要但罕见的异常类别。现有重平衡和对数调整方法主要关注类别频率，而忽略了不同心电图类别间方向依赖的形态变异性。

Method: 提出AG-SCL框架，集成三个组件：1) Angular Gaussian对比分支，在单位归一化嵌入上建模全协方差类不确定性；2) 自适应对数调整（Adaptive Logit Adjustment），学习有界标签状态特定的先验修正而非固定频率边界；3) 尾部感知增强，生成保持形态的视图同时保护7-25 Hz QRS主导频带。

Result: 在PTB-XL上，平衡准确率0.838、灵敏度0.709、特异度0.968、平均精度均值0.495、5%假阳性率下的真阳性率0.778。在Noc-ECG上，对应值为0.918、0.889、0.947、0.488、0.900。在罕见或形态不稳定的节律类别上提升最大。消融实验证实了各模块的贡献。

Conclusion: AG-SCL通过将先验校准与各向异性表示学习相结合，提升了长尾心电图诊断性能，增强了对罕见心律失常的敏感性，同时保持了临床相关的特异性。

Original Abstract: Long-tailed label distributions reduce the reliability of deep learning for electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia diagnosis, particularly for clinically important but rare abnormalities. Existing rebalancing and logit adjustment methods mainly address class frequency while overlooking direction-dependent morphological variability across ECG classes. This study proposes Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning (AG-SCL) for long-tailed multi-label ECG diagnosis. AG-SCL integrates three components into a unified framework: an Angular Gaussian contrastive branch that models full-covariance class uncertainty on unit-normalized embeddings, Adaptive Logit Adjustment that learns bounded label-state-specific prior corrections instead of fixed frequency-based margins, and tail-aware augmentation that generates morphology-preserving views while protecting the 7-25 Hz QRS-dominant band. The method was evaluated on the public PTB-XL benchmark and a nocturnal ECG dataset comprising 1317 hours of recordings from 141 subjects. AG-SCL achieved the best macro-level performance on both datasets. On PTB-XL, it obtained a balanced accuracy of 0.838, sensitivity of 0.709, specificity of 0.968, mean average precision of 0.495, and TPR at 5% FPR of 0.778. On Noc-ECG, the corresponding values were 0.918, 0.889, 0.947, 0.488, and 0.900. The largest gains occurred in rare or morphologically unstable rhythm classes, while ablation studies confirmed the contributions of full-covariance modelling, Adaptive Logit Adjustment, and tail-aware augmentation. AG-SCL improves long-tailed ECG diagnosis by combining prior calibration with anisotropic representation learning, enhancing sensitivity to rare arrhythmias while maintaining clinically relevant specificity. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Open-EXG/AG-SCL-for-Long-Tailed-ECG.

中文摘要翻译: 长尾标签分布降低了深度学习用于心电图心律失常诊断的可靠性，尤其是对临床重要但罕见的异常类别。现有重平衡和对数调整方法主要关注类别频率，而忽略了不同心电图类别间方向依赖的形态变异性。本研究提出Angular Gaussian监督对比学习（AG-SCL）用于长尾多标签心电图诊断。AG-SCL将三个组件集成到一个统一框架中：一个Angular Gaussian对比分支，在单位归一化嵌入上建模全协方差类不确定性；自适应对数调整，学习有界标签状态特定的先验修正而非固定频率边界；以及尾部感知增强，生成保持形态的视图同时保护7-25 Hz QRS主导频带。该方法在公开的PTB-XL基准数据集和一个包含141名受试者共1317小时记录的夜间心电图数据集上进行了评估。AG-SCL在两个数据集上均取得了最佳的宏观性能。在PTB-XL上，获得了0.838的平衡准确率、0.709的灵敏度、0.968的特异度、0.495的平均精度均值以及0.778的5%假阳性率下的真阳性率。在Noc-ECG上，对应值分别为0.918、0.889、0.947、0.488和0.900。最大的提升出现在罕见或形态不稳定的节律类别上，消融研究证实了全协方差建模、自适应对数调整和尾部感知增强的贡献。AG-SCL通过将先验校准与各向异性表示学习相结合，改善了长尾心电图诊断，增强了对罕见心律失常的敏感性，同时保持了临床相关的特异性。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/Open-EXG/AG-SCL-for-Long-Tailed-ECG。

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Entropy: Correctness-Aware Advantage Shaping via Contrastive Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14614)
*Weiwen Xu,Jia Liu,Hou Pong Chan,Long Li,Deng Cai,Min Chen,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比策略优化（CPO），利用参考引导分布与原始生成分布之间的对比不一致性替代熵，实现正确性感知的优势塑造，在RLVR中显著优于基于熵的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 熵无法区分有用不确定性和有害混乱，限制了其作为正确性信号的有效性，需要更好的正确性感知信号来塑造RLVR中的优势函数。

Method: 提出对比策略优化（CPO），使用参考引导分布与原始生成分布在token级别上的对比不一致性作为正确性感知的优势塑造信号，并从理论和实验上证明该不一致性可靠地指示token级别的正确性。

Result: 在域内和域外基准测试上，CPO大幅优于基于熵的RLVR方法，同时保持强泛化能力。进一步分析表明，正确和错误响应分别自然支持探索和利用，平衡两者可获得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 对比不一致性是一种比熵更有效的正确性信号，CPO作为通用框架（包含on-policy蒸馏作为特例）解决了零优势问题，在RLVR中取得了最优效果。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly uses entropy for advantage shaping. However, entropy cannot distinguish useful uncertainty from detrimental confusion, limiting its effectiveness as a correctness signal. We propose Contrastive Policy Optimization (CPO), which uses token-level contrastive disagreement between reference-guided and vanilla generation distributions for correctness-aware advantage shaping. Both theoretical and empirical results show that this disagreement reliably indicates token-level correctness. We further show that On-policy Distillation is a special case of CPO, where the posterior distribution is instantiated by an external teacher model. CPO also resolves the zero-advantage problem. Experiments on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that CPO substantially outperforms entropy-based RLVR methods while maintaining strong generalization. Further analysis shows that correct and incorrect responses naturally support exploration and exploitation respectively, and balancing both leads to the best performance.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通常使用熵进行优势塑造。然而，熵无法区分有用的不确定性和有害的混乱，这限制了其作为正确性信号的有效性。我们提出对比策略优化（CPO），该方法利用参考引导分布与原始生成分布之间的token级对比不一致性进行正确性感知的优势塑造。理论和实验结果均表明，这种不一致性可靠地指示了token级别上的正确性。我们进一步证明，在线蒸馏是CPO的一个特例，其中后验分布由外部教师模型实例化。CPO还解决了零优势问题。在域内和域外基准测试上的实验表明，CPO大幅优于基于熵的RLVR方法，同时保持强泛化能力。进一步分析显示，正确和错误的响应分别自然支持探索和利用，平衡两者可获得最佳性能。

</details>


### [140] [PolyQ: Codesigning End-to-End Quantization Framework for Scalable Edge CPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14618)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,KyungIn Nam,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyQ 是一种面向 CPU 的编译器与量化协同设计方案，通过逐通道比特分配（$\{2,3,4,8,16\}$）和编译时布局优化，实现低比特（3-6 bit）分数精度部署，在多个 CPU 平台上显著提升推理效率与困惑度，且能耗开销低于 2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有低比特量化方法要么只支持粗粒度的量化位宽，要么采用细粒度混合精度但难以在 CPU 上高效执行，缺乏一种既能实现细粒度比特分配又能高效部署在 CPU 上的实用方案。

Method: PolyQ 采用逐通道的激活感知比特分配（从 $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$ 中选择），通过编译时模型编译器将通道排列并聚类为比特同质块，生成兼容 SIMD 和 LUT 的内核，并跨算子合并兼容的排列以减少运行时布局重排开销。

Result: 在 Falcon-H1-3B、Llama2-13B 和 Qwen3-32B 上，PolyQ 在 3-6 bit 范围内提供稳定的质量缩放，在 3 bit 目标下困惑度相比先前方法提升 2.4%-32.1%。端到端测量显示，编译器布局重排减少激活重排流量最多 70.8%，预填延迟和解码吞吐量几乎与配置的比特预算成比例缩放，能耗/词元开销低于 2%。

Conclusion: 分数比特的 CPU 部署在实际场景中是可行、可预测且能效高的，能够适用于多种边缘目标设备。

Original Abstract: CPUs are the most universal target for on-device LLM inference, but existing low-bit quantization methods offer either coarse operating points or fine-grained mixed precision that is difficult to execute efficiently on CPUs. We present PolyQ, a CPU-oriented compiler/quantization co-design for activation-aware channel-wise bit allocation under a user-specified average-bit budget. PolyQ assigns per-channel bit-widths from $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$, then uses a compile-time model compiler to permute and cluster channels into bit-homogeneous blocks, generate SIMD- and LUT-compatible kernels, and merge compatible permutations across operators to keep layout regularization off the runtime path. This turns fine-grained budget fitting into a practical fractional-bit deployment method for CPU-only inference. Across Falcon-H1-3B, Llama2-13B, and Qwen3-32B on WikiText-2, PolyQ provides stable quality scaling from 3--6\,b and improves perplexity by 2.4--32.1\% over prior methods at a 3\,b target. End-to-end measurements on three representative CPUs -- workstation, laptop, and mobile -- show that compiler layout regularization reduces activation reorder traffic by up to 70.8\%, prefill latency and decode throughput scale nearly proportionally with the configured bit budget, and energy/token overhead stays below 2\% relative to an optimized LUT-based back-end. These results show that fractional-bit CPU deployment is practical, predictable, and energy-efficient across diverse edge targets.

中文摘要翻译: CPU 是设备端大语言模型推理最通用的目标平台，但现有的低比特量化方法要么只提供粗粒度的量化位宽，要么采用难以在 CPU 上高效执行的细粒度混合精度。我们提出了 PolyQ，一种面向 CPU 的编译器与量化协同设计方案，实现在用户指定的平均比特预算下进行激活感知的逐通道比特分配。PolyQ 为每个通道分配来自 $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$ 的比特宽度，然后通过编译时模型编译器将通道排列并聚类为比特同质块，生成兼容 SIMD 和查找表的内核，并跨算子合并兼容的排列，使布局重排脱离运行时路径。这将细粒度的预算拟合转化为一种适用于纯 CPU 推理的实用分数比特部署方法。在 WikiText-2 数据集上的 Falcon-H1-3B、Llama2-13B 和 Qwen3-32B 模型上，PolyQ 在 3-6 比特范围内提供了稳定的质量缩放，在 3 比特目标下困惑度相比先前方法提升 2.4%-32.1%。在三种代表性 CPU（工作站、笔记本电脑和移动设备）上的端到端测量表明，编译器布局重排将激活重排流量降低了最高 70.8%，预填延迟和解码吞吐量几乎与配置的比特预算成比例缩放，且相对于优化的基于查找表的后端，每个词元的能耗开销低于 2%。这些结果表明，分数比特的 CPU 部署在多种边缘目标设备上是实用、可预测且能效高的。

</details>


### [141] [TIDE: Trustworthy and Interpretable Battery Degradation Estimation with Contextual Learning and Symbolic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14640)
*Wen Yang Tan,Jiawei Li,Fang Liu,Wei Zhang,Sumei Sun,Peng Cheng Wang,Elisa Y. M. Ang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TIDE框架，融合电池领域知识与运行测量数据，实现电池健康估计的准确性、可信赖性和可解释性，并通过符号蒸馏提供模型级解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 电池健康估计在电池供电系统中至关重要，不准确的状态会影响控制、维护和使用寿命。在智能互联系统中，估计误差会在互联设备和下游决策中传播，因此需要同时保证准确性、可信赖性和可解释性的可靠估计方法。

Method: TIDE采用三组件骨干结构：知识引导的退化先验（提升可信赖性）、单调残差组件（提供与老化一致的可解释性细化）、上下文学习组件（捕获电池特定运行效应以提高准确性）。训练后的骨干通过符号蒸馏压缩成紧凑的符号代理模型，提供模型级可解释性。

Result: TIDE在估计精度上优于代表性基线方法19.7%；知识引导先验和单调残差建模显著减少了老化一致性违规；骨干支持组件级解释，符号蒸馏提供紧凑的模型级表示。

Conclusion: TIDE通过结合领域知识和数据驱动方法，在电池健康估计中实现了准确性、可信赖性和可解释性的统一，为智能互联系统中的电池健康监测和决策支持提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Battery health estimation is fundamental for battery management in battery-powered systems, where inaccurate health states may affect control, maintenance, and service life. It becomes even more critical in intelligent connected systems, where estimation errors can propagate across interconnected devices and downstream decisions. In this paper, we propose TIDE, a trustworthy and interpretable battery degradation estimator for reliable battery health estimation. TIDE jointly considers accuracy, trustworthiness, and interpretability, which are all essential for practical deployment and downstream decision making. To realize these objectives, TIDE combines battery-domain knowledge with operational measurements in a three-component backbone. A knowledge-guided degradation prior promotes trustworthy estimation, a monotone residual component provides interpretable aging-consistent refinement, and a contextual learning component captures battery-specific operational effects for improved accuracy. The trained backbone is then distilled into a compact symbolic surrogate that provides a concise model-level interpretation of its learned estimation logic. Experiments show that TIDE achieves strong estimation accuracy, improving overall estimation fidelity by an average of 19.7% over representative baselines. Its knowledge-guided prior and monotone residual modelling substantially reduce aging-consistency violations, supporting trustworthy estimation. Meanwhile, the backbone enables component-level interpretation, while symbolic distillation provides a compact model-level representation of the learned estimation logic. These results support the practical use of TIDE for battery health monitoring and decision support in intelligent connected systems.

中文摘要翻译: 电池健康估计是电池供电系统中电池管理的基础，不准确的健康状态可能会影响控制、维护和使用寿命。在智能互联系统中，这一问题变得尤为关键，因为估计误差会传播到互联设备和下游决策中。本文提出了TIDE——一种可信赖且可解释的电池退化估计器，用于可靠的电池健康估计。TIDE同时考虑准确性、可信赖性和可解释性，这些对于实际部署和下游决策都至关重要。为实现这些目标，TIDE将电池领域知识与运行测量数据结合在一个三组件骨干结构中：知识引导的退化先验促进可信赖的估计，单调残差组件提供与老化一致的可解释性细化，上下文学习组件捕获电池特定的运行效应以提高准确性。训练后的骨干被蒸馏为紧凑的符号代理模型，提供其学习到的估计逻辑的简洁模型级解释。实验表明，TIDE实现了强大的估计精度，相较于代表性基线方法，整体估计保真度平均提升了19.7%。其知识引导先验和单调残差建模大幅减少了老化一致性违规，支持可信赖的估计。同时，骨干结构支持组件级解释，而符号蒸馏提供了学习到的估计逻辑的紧凑模型级表示。这些结果支持TIDE在智能互联系统中用于电池健康监测和决策支持的实际应用。

</details>


### [142] [Trajectory-Aware Flow Matching for Topology Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14652)
*Shusheng Xiao,Jinshuai Bai,Hyogu Jeong,Yunfei Xi,Yilin Gui,YuanTong Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流匹配的拓扑优化（FMTO）框架，通过引入轨迹感知的流匹配方法，利用体积分数索引的BESO状态构建概率路径和目标速度场，实现了在少量采样步数下生成多样化、物理一致的高质量拓扑结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统拓扑优化依赖重复有限元分析和基于灵敏度的材料更新，在需生成多个候选设计时计算成本高昂。现有生成式拓扑优化方法存在对抗训练不稳定、反向扩散采样步数多或需外部引导等问题，难以兼顾结构可行性与物理一致性。

Method: 首先建立线性FMTO作为基线，在高斯源场与BESO参考拓扑之间插值。进一步提出轨迹感知FMTO，利用体积分数索引的BESO中间状态构建概率路径和目标速度场，将物理引导的优化历史嵌入生成式流学习，无需推理时额外优化。通过路径-速度失配分析解释轨迹权重对生成稳定性的影响。

Result: FMTO生成多样化的拓扑候选，在合规性性能、体积分数满足度、拓扑保真度方面均优于扩散基线，且采样步数大幅减少。在有限训练数据下，轨迹感知FMTO在适中轨迹权重下取得最佳综合性能。三维拓扑生成实验验证了框架的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的轨迹感知流匹配框架能有效学习物理引导的拓扑优化轨迹，实现高效、高质量的条件拓扑生成，且适用于三维问题。

Original Abstract: Topology optimisation (TO) often requires repeated finite element analysis and sensitivity-based material updates, which can be costly when multiple candidate designs are needed under varying physical and design conditions. Generative TO offers a route to rapid design exploration, but existing models may rely on adversarial training, long reverse-diffusion sampling, or external guidance to maintain structural feasibility and physical consistency. This study develops a flow matching-based topology optimisation (FMTO) framework for conditional topology generation. Linear FMTO is first formulated as an endpoint-based baseline by interpolating between a Gaussian source field and the BESO reference topology. To introduce mechanically meaningful intermediate states, a trajectory-aware FMTO formulation is proposed, where volume-fraction-indexed BESO states are used to construct the probability path and target velocity field. This incorporates physics-guided optimisation history into generative flow learning without adding inference-time optimisation. A path--velocity mismatch analysis explains why moderate trajectory weighting can improve generation stability, whereas excessive guidance may over-constrain the learned transport. Numerical examples show that FMTO generates diverse topology candidates with improved compliance-related performance, volume-fraction satisfaction, topology fidelity, and substantially fewer sampling steps than a diffusion-based baseline. Under limited training data, trajectory-aware FMTO achieves the best overall performance with a moderate trajectory weight. Studies on trajectory-anchor density and three-dimensional topology generation further demonstrate the influence of path design and the applicability of the proposed framework beyond two-dimensional problems.

中文摘要翻译: 拓扑优化（TO）通常需要重复进行有限元分析和基于灵敏度的材料更新，当需要在变化的物理和设计条件下生成多个候选设计时，这一过程可能成本高昂。生成式拓扑优化为快速设计探索提供了途径，但现有模型可能依赖于对抗训练、长反向扩散采样或外部引导来保持结构可行性和物理一致性。本研究开发了一个基于流匹配的拓扑优化（FMTO）框架，用于条件拓扑生成。首先将线性FMTO建立为基于端点的基线，在高斯源场与BESO参考拓扑之间进行插值。为了引入具有机械意义的中间状态，提出了一种轨迹感知的FMTO公式，其中使用体积分数索引的BESO状态来构建概率路径和目标速度场。这将物理引导的优化历史融入生成式流学习，而无需在推理时增加优化步骤。路径-速度失配分析解释了为何适度的轨迹加权能够提高生成稳定性，而过度的引导则可能过度约束所学习的传输过程。数值实验表明，FMTO能够生成多样化的拓扑候选，在合规性相关性能、体积分数满足度、拓扑保真度方面表现更优，且采样步数远少于基于扩散的基线方法。在有限训练数据下，轨迹感知FMTO在适中的轨迹权重下取得了最佳总体性能。轨迹锚点密度和三维拓扑生成的研究进一步展示了路径设计的影响以及所提框架在二维问题之外的适用性。

</details>


### [143] [Scalable Training of Continuous-Time Spiking Neural Networks with Differentiable Spike-Time Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14672)
*Yusuke Sakemi,Tomoya Takeuchi,Takeo Hosomi,Kazuyuki Aihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可微分尖峰时间离散化（DSTD）的高效内存训练框架，通过将不规则突触前尖峰映射到固定时间点的可微分加权事件，将连续时间SNN的激活内存从O(N_out N_in)降至O(N_out M)，并引入链式前馈神经簇启发的时序正则化，实现了高达100倍的内存节省和20倍的训练加速，成功在单GPU上训练9层CIFAR-10和20层Fashion-MNIST SNN。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 训练深度连续时间SNN受到精确尖峰时间计算所需内存的严重限制，该计算需要根据突触前尖峰排序在时间间隔内评估和保留候选放电时间，导致内存开销过大。

Method: 提出可微分尖峰时间离散化（DSTD）框架，将不规则突触前尖峰映射到固定时间点的可微分加权事件，用M个固定时间间隔替代输入相关的候选维度，同时精确近似连续时间膜电位动力学；并引入链式前馈神经簇启发的时序正则化，组织逐层放电窗口，缓解死亡神经元问题，支持流水线式处理。

Result: 在密集LIF层中，DSTD相比精确尖峰时间计算，峰值内存消耗降低约100倍，训练时间减少约20倍；成功在单GPU上训练了9层卷积SNN（CIFAR-10）和20层卷积SNN（Fashion-MNIST）。

Conclusion: DSTD框架通过离散化尖峰时间显著降低了连续时间SNN的训练内存和计算开销，结合时序正则化有效解决了深度SNN训练中的内存瓶颈和死亡神经元问题，为训练更深层SNN提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Continuous-time spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide an event-driven framework for temporal computation, computational neuroscience, and neuromorphic hardware. However, training deep continuous-time SNNs is severely constrained by the memory required for exact spike-time computation, which evaluates and retains candidate firing times over intervals determined by presynaptic spike ordering. Here we introduce a memory-efficient training framework based on differentiable spike-time discretization (DSTD) for leaky integrate-and-fire neurons with general membrane and synaptic time constants. DSTD maps irregular presynaptic spikes onto differentiable weighted events at fixed time points, replacing the input-dependent candidate dimension with $M$ fixed time intervals while accurately approximating continuous-time membrane-potential dynamics. This reduces candidate-related activation memory from $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}N_{\mathrm{in}})$ to $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}M)$ in the case of time-to-first-spike (TTFS) coding, where $N_{\mathrm{in}}$ and $N_{\mathrm{out}}$ denote the numbers of presynaptic and postsynaptic neurons, respectively. We further introduce synfire-chain-inspired temporal regularization that organizes layer-wise firing windows, mitigates dead-neuron failures, and enables pipeline-like processing. In dense LIF layers, DSTD reduced peak memory consumption by up to approximately 100-fold and training time by up to approximately 20-fold compared with exact spike-time computation. Together, these methods allowed us to train 9-layer convolutional SNNs on CIFAR-10 and 20-layer convolutional SNNs on Fashion-MNIST on a single GPU.
