中文摘要翻译: 连续时间脉冲神经网络（SNN）为时间计算、计算神经科学和神经形态硬件提供了事件驱动框架。然而，训练深度连续时间SNN受到精确尖峰时间计算所需内存的严重限制——该计算需要根据突触前尖峰排序，在时间间隔内评估并保留候选放电时间。本文针对具有一般膜时间常数和突触时间常数的泄露积分点火（LIF）神经元，提出了一种基于可微分尖峰时间离散化（DSTD）的高效内存训练框架。DSTD将不规则的突触前尖峰映射到固定时间点上的可微分加权事件，用M个固定时间间隔替代与输入相关的候选维度，同时精确近似连续时间膜电位动力学。在时间至首尖峰（TTFS）编码场景下，该方法将与候选相关的激活内存从O(N_out N_in)降至O(N_out M)，其中N_in和N_out分别表示突触前和突触后神经元的数量。我们进一步引入了受链式前馈神经簇启发的时序正则化方法，该方法组织逐层放电窗口，缓解死亡神经元故障，并支持流水线式处理。在密集LIF层中，与精确尖峰时间计算相比，DSTD将峰值内存消耗降低了约100倍，训练时间减少了约20倍。综合以上方法，我们能够在单个GPU上训练9层卷积SNN处理CIFAR-10数据集，以及20层卷积SNN处理Fashion-MNIST数据集。

</details>


### [144] [Grad2Fair: A Gradient-driven Approach for Graph Fairness without Demographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14705)
*Yuchang Zhu,Zezhong Xie,Huizhe Zhang,Huazhen Zhong,Jintang Li,Liang Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人口统计信息的图神经网络公平性方法Grad2Fair，利用梯度分布中的隐式人口统计信息来量化并缓解群体偏见，避免使用不准确的预测人口统计信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图神经网络公平性方法大多假设人口统计信息完全可用，而部分使用预测人口统计信息的方法因预测不准确而导致公平性提升失败。本文旨在探索无需人口统计信息即可实现图公平性的方法。

Method: 首先提出GradDist，一种基于梯度的度量指标，通过测量误分类节点梯度分布中局部模式之间的距离来量化偏见。然后提出Grad2Fair，一种梯度引导的公平性方法，直接利用梯度进行去偏，避免人口统计信息预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Grad2Fair在大多数情况下优于现有基线方法，具有稳定的公平性表现。

Conclusion: Grad2Fair通过利用梯度中隐含的人口统计信息，有效解决了无人口统计信息情况下的图公平性问题，避免了预测人口统计信息带来的不稳定性。

Original Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) frequently encounter group fairness issues, often yielding biased predictions against specific demographic groups defined by sensitive attributes such as gender or race. While this challenge has motivated extensive research, most existing solutions rely on the strong assumption that demographics are fully available. To bypass this strict requirement, a few recent studies have attempted to use predicted demographics as proxies to enforce fairness constraints. However, predicted demographics may be inaccurate, resulting in the failure to improve fairness. In this work, we investigate the problem of graph fairness without demographic information and avoid the utilization of predicted demographics. Motivated by our observation that the gradient distributions of misclassified nodes implicitly encode demographic information, we first propose GradDist, a gradient-based metric that quantifies bias by measuring the distance between local modes within these distributions. To mitigate this bias, we propose Gradient-to-Fairness (Grad2Fair), a gradient-guided approach for group fairness without demographics. Due to the potential demographics in gradients, Grad2Fair directly leverages gradients to debias and eliminates demographic prediction, thereby enabling stable fairness performance. Experiments on several real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of Grad2Fair, as evidenced by superior performance over baselines in most cases. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZzoomD/Grad2Fair.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络（GNN）经常面临群体公平性问题，常会针对由性别或种族等敏感属性定义的特定人口群体产生有偏见的预测。尽管这一挑战已引发广泛研究，但大多数现有解决方案依赖于人口统计信息完全可用的强假设。为了绕过这一严格限制，少数近期研究尝试使用预测的人口统计信息作为代理来施加公平性约束。然而，预测的人口统计信息可能不准确，导致公平性提升失败。在这项工作中，我们研究了无需人口统计信息的图公平性问题，并避免使用预测的人口统计信息。受我们观察到误分类节点的梯度分布隐含编码了人口统计信息这一现象的启发，我们首先提出了GradDist，一种基于梯度的度量指标，通过测量这些分布中局部模式之间的距离来量化偏见。为了缓解这种偏见，我们提出了Gradient-to-Fairness（Grad2Fair），一种无需人口统计信息的梯度引导的群体公平性方法。由于梯度中潜在的人口统计信息，Grad2Fair直接利用梯度进行去偏并消除了人口统计信息预测，从而实现了稳定的公平性表现。在多个真实世界数据集上的实验证明了Grad2Fair的有效性，在大多数情况下其性能优于基线方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/ZzoomD/Grad2Fair获取。

</details>


### [145] [MESHA: Mechanism-Enforced Sequential Halving for Strategic Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14706)
*Xin Li,Zixin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MESHA算法，用于战略线性老虎机中的最佳臂识别问题。该算法结合均匀采样和周期性格里姆触发条件，应对臂可能策略性误报特征向量的行为，理论分析和实验均表明其优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在战略线性老虎机设定中，每个臂可能通过策略性地误报其特征向量来最大化自己被识别为最佳臂的概率，而奖励由臂的真实但不可观测的特征生成。现有基于G-最优设计的线性BAI算法在这种战略环境下会失效，因为基于策略性报告特征的最优设计采样规则可能导致最优臂被"饿死"（得不到任何采样预算）。因此需要设计能抵御策略性行为的BAI算法。

Method: MESHA算法采用两种核心机制：(1) 朴素均匀采样规则，用于减少臂策略性行为的影响；(2) 周期性格里姆触发条件（GTC），用于消除报告特征严重偏离真实特征的臂。算法在任意纳什均衡下进行分析。

Result: 理论方面：证明任何臂都会试图通过GTC检查以最大化被识别概率，并给出了MESHA在固定预算T下的失败概率上界；同时证明了现有的基于G-最优设计的线性BAI算法在战略环境下会失败。实验方面：大量数值实验表明MESHA优于依赖最优设计采样规则的基线和特征无关的基线方法。

Conclusion: MESHA通过均匀采样和周期性格里姆触发条件的结合，有效地解决了战略线性老虎机中的最佳臂识别问题，在理论保证和实验性能上均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: We design and analyze \underline{M}echanism-\underline{E}nforced \underline{S}equential \underline{HA}lving (MESHA), an algorithm for Best Arm Identification (BAI) in strategic linear bandits. In this setting, each arm may strategically misreport its feature vector to maximize the probability of being identified as the best arm, when rewards are generated from the arms' true but unobservable features. The design of MESHA applies the naïve uniform sampling rule and an epoch-wise Grim Trigger Condition (GTC): the former reduces the impact of arms' strategic behaviours and the latter eliminates arms whose reported features severely deviate from the ground truth. Considering an arbitrary Nash Equilibrium, we prove that any arm would attempt to pass the GTC check to maximize its identified probability and derive an upper bound on the failure probability of MESHA within a fixed budget $T$. We also show that state-of-the-art linear BAI algorithms with $G$-optimal design would fail in such strategic environment, as the optimal design (OD)-based sampling rule based on strategically reported features may {\it starve} the optimal arm of any sampling budget. Finally, extensive numerical experiments indicate that MESHA
  outperforms baselines that rely on OD-based sampling rules as well as the feature-agnostic baselines, corroborating the efficacy of MESHA.

中文摘要翻译: 我们设计并分析了\underline{M}echanism-\underline{E}nforced \underline{S}equential \underline{HA}lving（MESHA），一种用于战略线性老虎机中最佳臂识别（BAI）的算法。在该设定中，每个臂可能策略性地误报其特征向量，以最大化自己被识别为最佳臂的概率，而奖励由臂的真实但不可观测的特征生成。MESHA的设计采用了朴素均匀采样规则和周期性格里姆触发条件（GTC）：前者减少臂策略性行为的影响，后者消除报告特征严重偏离真实特征的臂。考虑任意纳什均衡，我们证明任何臂都会试图通过GTC检查以最大化其被识别的概率，并推导出MESHA在固定预算T内的失败概率上界。我们还证明，当前最先进的使用G-最优设计的线性BAI算法在这种战略环境下会失败，因为基于策略性报告特征的最优设计（OD）采样规则可能导致最优臂"饿死"——得不到任何采样预算。最后，大量数值实验表明，MESHA优于依赖最优设计采样规则的基线以及特征无关的基线，验证了MESHA的有效性。

</details>


### [146] [Counterfactuals for Feature-Weighted Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14719)
*Richard J. Fawley,Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VoICE框架针对加权k-means聚类提出基于Voronoi图的反事实解释方法，通过投影到目标聚类的加权Voronoi区域生成最小代价解释，并引入可行动性约束、数据边界和同伦收缩，比现有基线方法更稳定地产生有效目标簇归属。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有反事实解释在监督学习中已成熟，但扩展到聚类场景面临挑战：聚类分配无标签且受分区几何结构支配。现有方法仅考虑单一质心边界对，缺乏特征权重感知，无法生成满足可行动性约束且简洁的解释。

Method: VoICE框架将反事实生成建模为向目标簇完整加权Voronoi区域的正交投影，将特征权重直接融入聚类几何和反事实目标函数；将目标区域与数据导出边界相交，并通过同伦收缩（homothetic contraction）向质心收缩，以限制外推和边界敏感性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，VoICE始终生成有效的目标簇成员归属，而主流成对基线方法无法做到。

Conclusion: VoICE通过加权Voronoi区域的完整建模，相比仅考虑成对质心边界的方法，能更可靠地为聚类分配生成有效、可解释且满足可行动性约束的反事实解释。

Original Abstract: Counterfactual explanations provide local, interpretable insight by identifying changes to an input that would alter its assigned outcome. Although well established in supervised learning, their extension to clustering is less direct, since cluster assignments are unlabeled and governed by the geometry of the partition. This paper introduces VoICE, a Voronoi-Induced Counterfactual Explainability framework for feature-weighted $k$-means clustering. Rather than treating cluster change as a crossing of a single pairwise centroid boundary, VoICE formulates counterfactual generation as projection onto the full weighted Voronoi region of a target cluster, incorporating feature weights directly into both the clustering geometry and the counterfactual objective to yield least-cost and parsimonious explanations under actionability constraints. Target regions are further intersected with data-derived bounds and homothetically contracted towards their centroids, limiting extrapolation and boundary sensitivity. VoICE consistently produces valid target-cluster membership, across several benchmark datasets, where the leading pairwise baseline does not.

中文摘要翻译: 反事实解释通过识别输入中能够改变其分配结果的更改，提供局部、可解释的洞见。尽管该方法在监督学习中已十分成熟，但其在聚类中的扩展却不够直接，因为聚类分配是无标签的，且受分区几何结构支配。本文提出VoICE——一种面向特征加权k-means聚类的Voronoi诱导反事实可解释性框架。VoICE没有将聚类变化视为跨越单一成对质心边界，而是将反事实生成建模为向目标簇完整加权Voronoi区域的正交投影，将特征权重直接融入聚类几何和反事实目标函数中，从而在可行动性约束下产生最小代价且简洁的解释。目标区域进一步与数据导出边界相交，并通过同伦收缩向质心收缩，以限制外推和边界敏感性。在多个基准数据集上，VoICE始终生成有效的目标簇成员归属，而主流的成对基线方法无法做到。

</details>


### [147] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般凸目标下局部SGD（联邦平均）在二阶异质性假设下的收敛性猜想，并给出了紧的下界，完善了局部SGD的收敛理论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 虽然局部SGD在实际中表现优异，但理论仅部分解释了其在数据异质性下何时及为何有效。Patel等人[2025]证明了强凸目标下二阶异质性假设能刻画局部SGD的效率，并推测该原理可推广到一般凸情形。本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 本文通过建立改进的收敛性保证，证明了一般凸目标下局部SGD在二阶有界异质性下的收敛性；同时改进了该设置下局部SGD的已知下界，证明上界接近最优。

Result: 1. 证明了一般凸目标下局部SGD在二阶有界异质性下的改进收敛保证；2. 改进了该设置下局部SGD的最佳已知下界，证明上界近乎紧；3. 作为技术延伸，给出了带替换的串行SGD的下界，表明二阶异质性能够刻画高曲率稀有客户端的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供了更精细的局部SGD收敛理论，证实了二阶异质性假设在一般凸情形下同样有效，且理论上下界近乎匹配，为理解局部更新在数据异质性下的优势提供了更完整的理论支撑。

Original Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting. In this paper, we prove this conjecture by establishing an improved convergence guarantee for Local SGD on general convex objectives under bounded second-order heterogeneity. We also improve the best-known lower bounds for Local SGD in this setting, showing that our upper bounds are nearly tight. Together, these results provide a sharper, more fine-grained convergence theory for Local SGD. As a further application of our techniques, we provide a lower bound for serial SGD with replacement, showing how second-order heterogeneity captures the impact of rare high-curvature clients.

中文摘要翻译: 局部SGD（也称为联邦平均）是一种广泛使用的分布式优化算法。尽管局部SGD在实际中通常优于小批量SGD等替代方案，但理论仍仅部分解释了在现实数据异质性下局部更新何时以及为何有效。Patel等人[2025]的最新工作表明，有界二阶异质性假设能够刻画强凸目标下局部SGD的效率，并推测该原理可推广到一般凸情形。在本文中，我们证明了这一猜想，在一般凸目标下建立了局部SGD在二阶有界异质性下的改进收敛保证。我们还改进了该设置下局部SGD的最佳已知下界，表明我们的上界近乎紧。这些结果共同为局部SGD提供了更清晰、更精细的收敛理论。作为我们技术的进一步应用，我们给出了带替换的串行SGD的下界，展示了二阶异质性如何刻画高曲率稀有客户端的影响。

</details>


### [148] [GAttNHP: Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process for Extrapolation Reasoning in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14733)
*Xiangni Tian,Kaixian Yu,Runpeng Dai,Niansheng Tang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分组注意力神经霍克斯过程（GAttNHP）的统一框架，通过自注意力编码器、语义软分组模块和非交叉分位数回归头，同时解决时序知识图谱中长程依赖、事件链间互激励/抑制及重尾分布时间预测三个难题，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TKG预测面临三个核心挑战：(i) 难以编码长程时间依赖；(ii) 不同事件链之间相互激励或抑制，快照级模型无法表达这种交互；(iii) 事件间隔时间呈重尾分布且统计稀疏，确定性时间预测器不可靠。现有方法未能同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 提出GAttNHP框架，包含三个组件：(1) 自注意力编码器，将每个主-关系链建模为连续时间点过程，捕捉远端历史的持续激励；(2) 语义软分组模块，将全局可学习的霍克斯先验转化为分析性交叉注意力掩码，通过潜在分组隶属关系共享激励模式；(3) 非交叉分位数回归头（NCQ），替代均值预测，提供校准的、单调有序的分位数估计，在重尾分布下保持稳定。

Result: 在六个TKG基准数据集上，GAttNHP在实体预测和时间预测任务上均优于最先进基线；消融实验表明，最大的性能提升出现在长尾事件链上——即现有模型失败最严重的场景。

Conclusion: GAttNHP通过将神经霍克斯过程与分组注意力机制及分位数回归相结合，有效统一解决了TKG预测中的长程依赖、事件链间交互和重尾时间分布三大难题，尤其擅长处理长尾事件链预测。

Original Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs) record how facts evolve over time, but forecasting future events on a TKG remains difficult for three reasons: (i) long-range temporal dependencies are hard to encode; (ii) events on different chains mutually excite or inhibit one another in ways that snapshot-level models cannot express; and (iii) inter-arrival times are heavy-tailed and statistically sparse, so deterministic time predictors are unreliable. We address these three issues with a single framework, the \textbf{Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process (GAttNHP)}, built around three matched components. First, a self-attention encoder casts each subject--relation chain as a continuous-time point process and captures the lingering excitation of distant history. Second, a semantic soft-grouping module turns globally learnable Hawkes priors into an analytical cross-attention mask, so chains share excitation patterns through their latent group memberships rather than through exhaustive pairwise computation. Third, a Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) regression head replaces mean-based time prediction, providing calibrated, monotonically ordered quantile estimates that remain stable under heavy-tailed inter-arrival distributions. On six benchmark TKG datasets, GAttNHP improves over state-of-the-art baselines on both entity prediction and time prediction, and ablations confirm that its largest gains arise on the long-tail event chains where existing models fail most severely.

中文摘要翻译: 时序知识图谱（TKG）记录了事实随时间演化的情况，但在TKG上预测未来事件仍然存在三个难点：(i) 长程时间依赖难以编码；(ii) 不同链条上的事件之间会相互激励或抑制，快照级模型无法表达这种关系；(iii) 事件间隔时间呈重尾分布且统计稀疏，因此确定性时间预测器不可靠。我们通过一个统一框架——\textbf{分组注意力神经霍克斯过程（GAttNHP）}——来解决这三个问题，该框架由三个匹配的组件构成。首先，自注意力编码器将每个主体-关系链建模为连续时间点过程，捕捉远端历史的持续激励。其次，语义软分组模块将全局可学习的霍克斯先验转化为分析性的交叉注意力掩码，使得各链条通过其潜在分组隶属关系共享激励模式，而无需进行穷举的成对计算。第三，非交叉分位数（NCQ）回归头替代了基于均值的时间预测，提供校准的、单调有序的分位数估计，在重尾间隔分布下保持稳定。在六个TKG基准数据集上，GAttNHP在实体预测和时间预测任务上均优于最先进的基线方法，消融实验证实，其最大的性能提升出现在现有模型失败最严重的长尾事件链上。

</details>


### [149] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoQG，首个面向时态知识图谱问答生成的基准构建框架，并揭示现有方法在保留时态约束方面的不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有知识图谱问题生成（KGQG）基准大多基于静态知识图谱，未编码图事实的时态范围，因此无法评估生成问题是否忠实保留了时态有效性、事件顺序和答案决定的时态约束。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究时态知识图谱问题生成（TKGQG）。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，包含三个方面：（1）综合的时态约束分类体系；（2）拓扑-时态子图采样；（3）基于痕迹的问题生成。通过该框架从异质时态知识图谱构建了四个基准数据集，共16,011个经过验证的问题。

Result: 评估了代表性基于LLM的KGQG方法和提示基线，结果表明现有方法在保留时态约束方面存在困难，特别是在多约束设置和更难的时态约束类型下表现不佳。

Conclusion: 揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间的明显差距，并将ChronoQG确立为时态忠实问题生成的挑战性测试基准。

Original Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

中文摘要翻译: 知识图谱问题生成（KGQG）旨在从结构化图证据中生成自然语言问题。然而，现有的KGQG基准大多基于静态知识图谱构建，未编码图事实的时态范围。因此，它们无法评估生成问题是否忠实保留了时态有效性、事件顺序以及决定答案的时态约束。本文研究时态知识图谱问题生成（TKGQG），其中生成的问题必须同时忠实于支持子图和识别目标答案所需的时态约束。我们提出ChronoQG，这是首个具有时态表达能力和跳数约束的TKGQG基准构建框架。ChronoQG集成了综合的时态约束分类体系、拓扑-时态子图采样以及基于痕迹的问题生成，以构建时态忠实的问题。该框架从异质时态知识图谱中构建了四个基准数据集，共计16,011个经过验证的问题。我们评估了代表性基于LLM的KGQG方法和提示基线在多种TKGQG设置（包括时态约束数量、拓扑模板和时态约束类型）下的表现。结果表明，现有方法难以保留时态约束，尤其是在多约束设置和更难的时态约束类型下。这些发现揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间的明显差距，并将ChronoQG确立为时态忠实问题生成的挑战性测试基准。

</details>


### [150] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于决策理论的 epistemic uncertainty 评估框架，证明最优选择器是 aleatoric 和 epistemic 不确定性的阈值化凸组合，并揭示了现有不确定性解耦文献中相关性指标无法反映实际效用的缺陷，进而提出用风险-遗憾-覆盖率曲面作为解耦质量的诊断工具。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 epistemic uncertainty 评估依赖于 OOD 检测和主动学习等代理任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用的不确定性评分并不一致，亟需一种基于决策理论的一致性评估方法。

Method: 基于 epistemic reject-option 框架，将选择性预测建模为关于覆盖率、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题；证明最优选择器是真实 aleatoric 和 epistemic 不确定性的阈值化凸组合；提出用可达到的风险、遗憾、覆盖率曲面作为解耦效用的诊断指标。

Result: 在密集人工标注的数据集上，决策理论排名与代理任务排名存在显著差异，包括排名倒置——某些方法在一个标准下排名最高，在另一个标准下排名最低。

Conclusion: 标准相关性指标无法预测解耦组件的实际操作效用；决策理论视角下的评估框架能更真实地反映不确定性解耦方法的性能。

Original Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

中文摘要翻译: 当前对认知不确定性的评估依赖于分布外检测和主动学习等任务。然而，这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用量化认知不确定性的评分并不一致。基于认知拒绝选项框架，我们利用认知不确定性识别遗憾（即可减少误差）的能力来评估它。将选择性预测建模为关于覆盖率、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，我们证明最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合。这一理论统一揭示了近期不确定性解耦文献中的一个缺陷：我们证明了学习组件之间的标准相关性指标并不一定能预测其实际的操作效用。我们转而提出评估分解的可达到的风险、遗憾和覆盖率曲面，作为联合解耦和效用的诊断工具。在带有密集人工标注的数据集上对标准方法进行基准测试表明，决策理论排名与代理任务排名可能存在显著差异，包括在某些标准上排名最高的方法在其他标准上排名最低的成对排名倒置。

</details>


### [151] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非对称峰值感知损失（APAL），针对时间序列预测中低估惩罚更重、峰值区域训练权重更高的需求，在多个骨干网络上提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在许多时间序列预测应用中（如人群需求预测），低估风险远高于高估，且罕见需求峰值预测对下游任务至关重要。但现有模型多用对称损失函数（如MSE、MAE）并以聚合误差评估，无法捕捉极端值和峰值时序预测的失败。

Method: 提出APAL损失函数，包含两个关键设计：(i) 对低估施加更大惩罚；(ii) 增加每个预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE基础上补充通道级尾部误差（Top-10%和Top-1%）及峰值指标（精度、召回率、时序容忍度下的F1和峰值时序误差）。

Result: 在五个先进骨干网络上，基于墨尔本行人计数和海滩游客计数等峰值关键数据集，APAL提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，同时暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: 当峰值预测失败是主要操作关切时，APAL提供了一种实用的解决方案，在峰值关键场景下优于对称损失函数。

Original Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多操作性的时间序列预测应用中，例如人群需求预测，低估相关的风险远高于高估。准确预测罕见的需求峰值在下游任务中起着关键作用。然而，大多数时间序列预测器使用对称目标函数（如MSE、MAE）进行训练，并主要基于聚合误差进行评估，这可能会掩盖极端值和峰值时序预测的失败。我们引入了非对称峰值感知损失（APAL），一种简单、模型无关的目标函数，它（i）对低估施加更重的惩罚，（ii）增加每个预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。我们进一步提出了一种峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE的基础上补充通道级尾部误差（Top-10%和Top-1%）和峰值指标（精度、召回率、时序容忍度下的F1以及峰值时序误差）。我们使用五个最先进的骨干网络在长期多变量预测上评估APAL，重点关注行人需求预测，使用了（i）墨尔本市行人小时计数数据集的生产级子集和（ii）海滩游客计数数据集。该损失函数在时间序列预测上的通用性在额外基准上进行了测试。在峰值关键的数据集和设置中，APAL提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，同时暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡，使其在峰值预测失败是主要操作关切时成为一种实用的解决方案。

</details>


### [152] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标下的学习问题，提出了一种去中心化、私有信息下的PAC学习算法，并引入了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，证明了多项式样本复杂度界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有关于TBSG的PAC学习工作依赖于两个强假设：(a) 双方共享公开信息；(b) 双方使用相同的集中式学习算法。本文旨在放松这些假设，研究在私有信息和去中心化学习（双方使用不同算法）条件下是否仍能实现可达性目标的学习。

Method: (1) 放松了公开信息和集中式学习的假设，设计了在私有信息和非共享算法下的去中心化学习方案；(2) 引入博弈论版本的期望条件距离（ECD）参数，用于衡量到达目标集的期望步数，并基于该参数建立了多项式样本复杂度界。

Result: 首次证明了在去中心化和私有信息条件下，TBSG中可达性目标的可学习性；建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数、误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度界。

Conclusion: 本文是首个在去中心化和私有信息设置下，针对TBSG中可达性目标给出正面学习结果的工作，为更现实的多人博弈学习场景提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

中文摘要翻译: 可达性是最基本的逻辑目标，但在强化学习设置中，众所周知难以学习：即使是马尔可夫决策过程，在无额外假设下也无法PAC学习可达性。这一困难同样存在于回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中，其中两个对抗性玩家在有限状态空间上交互。本文考虑具有可达性目标的回合制随机博弈。在此类设置中，对抗性学习（即玩家在学习阶段也是对抗性的）是不可能的。因此，目标是考虑两个玩家一起学习未知模型的学习方式。基于此精神，先前关于TBSG中PAC学习的文献假设：(a) 双方共享公开信息；(b) 集中式学习，即双方使用相同的学习算法。本文的贡献有两方面。首先，我们放松这些强假设并确保学习的可行性：(i) 使用不与对方共享的私有信息；(ii) 去中心化学习，即双方不使用相同的学习算法。据我们所知，本文是首个针对具有可达性目标的TBSG，在去中心化和私有信息学习方面给出正面结果的工作。其次，我们引入了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，该参数衡量到达目标集的期望长度。我们建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数以及误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度界。

</details>


### [153] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现，在狭窄的、经过事实校验和合规审核的数据集上微调语言模型，会导致模型在无关领域（如刑事司法、环境、文化品味等）上产生广泛的意识形态偏移，同时保持通用能力不变。作者将这一现象称为"意识形态泛化"，并提出了衡量其广度与放大效应的方法论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究在狭窄、精心筛选的数据集上微调语言模型是否会导致超出训练领域的、广泛的意识形态偏移，从而揭示微调数据选择可能带来的隐性风险。

Method: 在GPT-4.1和Gemma-3上分别使用左倾和右倾的经济学Q&A数据、职场HR政策数据、金融查询数据以及食品安全数据（科学-伪科学轴）进行微调，然后评估模型在无关领域（刑事司法、环境、文化品味等）上的立场偏移，并比较few-shot prompting与微调的效果差异。

Result: 微调在狭窄数据上会导致模型在无关领域产生一致的意识形态偏移；few-shot prompting可以指示偏移方向，但微调会将模型推向更极端的位置，包括种族-IQ关联和政治暴力等远超出分布的输出；该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无裁判评估和外部基准下依然成立，与通用数据混合后仍然存在，且GSM8K精度变化在±1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 窄域微调会导致跨领域的意识形态泛化，且微调具有比few-shot更强的放大效应，这可能带来安全隐患，即使训练数据本身看似无害。

Original Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 在小型、精心策划的数据集上微调语言模型，是使其适应特定策略或领域的标准做法。我们证明，在狭窄的、事实上有据可查且符合审核标准的数据上进行微调，会在无关领域引发广泛的意识形态偏移，同时保留通用能力。在右倾或左倾的经济学问答数据上训练GPT-4.1，会在刑事司法、环境和文化品味等主题上产生匹配的意识形态偏移。同样的效应出现在可能被实际部署的数据集上，例如职场HR政策和实用金融查询，以及科学-伪科学轴上——食物安全微调会增加模型对表达虚假健康信念的用户的谄媚性认同。我们将这一现象称为意识形态泛化，并提出了一种方法论来测量两个属性：广度（偏移跨越训练中未涉及主题的程度）和放大效应（微调相对于在相同例子上进行少样本提示所加剧的偏移程度）。我们表明，少样本提示可以指示泛化的方向，但微调会将模型推向更极端的程度，包括远超出分布的输出，如对种族-IQ关联和政治暴力的认可。该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无裁判评估和外部基准下依然成立，与通用数据混合后仍然存在，且GSM8K精度变化在基线水平的±1个百分点以内。

</details>


### [154] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于真值表逻辑结构化的二分类器最优线性组合方法，建立了凸化经验风险最小点存在性与唯一性的充分条件，并在三分类器情形下枚举了所有解的类型，推导了指数损失和对数损失下最优权重的显式解析公式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当使用多个二分类器进行集成时，如何最优地线性组合它们是一个基础问题。现有方法通常依赖迭代优化，缺乏对解的存在性、唯一性以及全局最优性的严格理论分析，尤其是在分类器数量较多时。

Method: 利用真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，将分类器对数据的划分转化为等价类；通过分类校准函数的多元推广，对凸化经验风险进行严格分析；建立了最优权重存在性与唯一性的充分条件；针对三分类器情形枚举了所有配置类型；采用指数损失（Boost）和对数损失（Logit）推导了最优权重的显式解析公式。

Result: 给出了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险全局最小点存在且唯一的充分条件；在三分类器情形下完整枚举了唯一解、下确界和非唯一最小点三类配置；得到了指数损失和对数损失下最优权重的闭合解析表达式；提出了φ-前沿概念用于评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上解决了多分类器线性组合的最优权重分析问题，避免了迭代优化，为集成学习提供了严格的数学基础。所提出的φ-前沿概念也为模型诊断和数据质量评估提供了新工具。

Original Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究基于真值表逻辑结构化的二分类器最优线性组合问题。给定的分类器将数据划分为等价类，通过分类校准函数的多元推广，可以对凸化经验风险进行严格分析。我们建立了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险（全局）最小点存在且唯一的充分条件（当分类器数量较大时，常常可能不存在最小点）。在三分类器情形下，我们的分析完整枚举了导致唯一解、下确界或非唯一最小点的所有配置。此外，我们利用指数损失（Boost）和对数损失（Logit）推导了最优权重的显式解析公式，无需迭代优化。通过引入φ-前沿的概念，可以评估所得分类器的稳定性以及数据质量。

</details>


### [155] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本方法，通过指令微调后与原始推理模型融合，利用有监督微调数据进一步提升推理语言模型（RLM）在可验证和难验证领域的性能，成本不到3美元。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLM在数学和编码等可验证领域表现出色，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域训练仍具挑战；同时，大量有监督微调数据（含人工编写的解决方案）未被充分利用于RLM的进一步优化。

Method: 首先对RLM进行经典的指令微调（无推理轨迹的监督微调），然后将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行融合（模型合并），恢复其在目标领域的推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证和难验证领域（包括编码和文本摘要）均提升了RLM性能，同时保持了RLM在其他领域的能力，且成本极低（不到3美元）。

Conclusion: 表明通过指令微调与模型融合的方式，可以高效利用已有的有监督微调数据来改进RLM，且方法具有高度成本效益。

Original Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

中文摘要翻译: 推理语言模型（RLM）在数学和编码等领域展现了令人印象深刻的性能。这些领域允许对模型输出进行可靠验证，这对于驱动RLM性能提升的强化学习至关重要。然而，在缺乏可靠验证器的领域训练RLM仍然具有挑战性。与此同时，对于可验证和不可验证的领域，都存在大量未使用的、含有人工编写解决方案的有监督微调数据。在这项工作中，我们表明这些数据可以被高效利用以进一步提升RLM性能。为此，我们首先在RLM上使用经典的指令微调（即不含推理轨迹的有监督微调）。接着，我们将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行融合，恢复其在目标领域上的推理行为。我们的广泛评估表明，我们的方法在可验证和难验证领域（包括编码和文本摘要）均能提升RLM性能，同时保持RLM在其他领域的能力。重要的是，我们的方法具有极高的成本效益，实现这样的改进花费不到3美元。

</details>


### [156] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机化Logit缩放的即插即用黑盒防御方法(RLS)，能在保持模型准确率的同时有效降低黑盒得分攻击的成功率，并设计了一种自适应攻击揭示现有非随机化防御的脆弱性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对抗性样本对机器学习模型在真实场景的可靠部署构成严重威胁，尤其黑盒场景下需要可扩展、低成本且强大的防御方法。

Method: 提出Random Logit Scaling (RLS)，一种基于随机化的后处理防御方法：通过随机缩放Logit输出混淆攻击者，在保持模型准确率的同时最小化置信度失真。

Result: RLS显著降低了最先进黑盒得分攻击的成功率，相比现有随机化防御方法在保持准确率和最小化置信度失真方面更优；此外，针对非随机化防御AAA的自适应攻击揭示了其脆弱性。

Conclusion: RLS是一种高效、即插即用的黑盒防御方法，能够在不牺牲模型准确率的前提下有效抵御黑盒对抗攻击，同时证明了现有非随机化防御在面对自适应攻击时存在脆弱性。

Original Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习模型越来越多地被应用于各个领域。然而，对抗性样本对这类模型的可靠部署构成了重大威胁。近年来，一些强大的对抗性样本攻击方法被提出，能够快速且查询高效地生成对抗性样本，即使在黑盒场景下也是如此，这凸显了对可扩展、低成本且强大防御方法的需求。在本文中，我们针对黑盒对抗性样本攻击与防御领域做出了两项贡献。首先，我们提出了随机Logit缩放（RLS），一种针对黑盒基于得分的对抗性攻击的基于随机化的防御方法。RLS是一种即插即用的后处理防御方法，可以以最小的工作量在任意现有机器学习模型之上实现。RLS的核心思想是通过输出由随机缩放Logit产生的伪造得分来混淆攻击者，同时保持模型准确率。我们证明，与最先进的基于随机化的防御方法相比，RLS在保持准确率并最小化置信度失真的同时，显著降低了最先进黑盒得分攻击的成功率。其次，我们引入了一种针对AAA的新型自适应攻击，AAA是一种最先进的针对黑盒得分攻击的非随机化黑盒防御方法，它也通过修改输出Logit来混淆攻击者，我们的实验证明了AAA在面对自适应攻击时的脆弱性。

</details>


### [157] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代简化SOTA模型DynaMix，提出了仅含两个可解释参数的极简模型DynaBase，该模型在动力系统零样本重建上表现优异，并揭示了上下文学习所需的最小机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基础模型在零样本动力系统重建中表现强劲，但其内部机制不透明，难以揭示动力系统领域上下文学习所需的最小必要条件。本文旨在通过极端简化模型架构，暴露其核心机制。

Method: 将SOTA模型DynaMix迭代简化为仅含两个可解释参数的最小形式DynaBase，其预测通过当前潜在状态与最近上下文邻居及其时序后继的线性混合实现。

Result: DynaBase尽管极度简单，但在混沌和周期系统的零样本动力系统重建上具有高度竞争力，参数量比其它基础模型低多个数量级；支持对动力系统重建指标的直接模型优化及预测MSE的闭式单步解析解；进一步导出一个单参数映射族，一端对应上下文模仿算法，另一端呈现混沌行为。

Conclusion: DynaBase揭示了产生零样本动力系统重建所需的最小机制，并在一个易理解的数学框架内统一了文献中的不同观察结果，同时展示了不同训练策略如何导致模型在短期预测或动力系统重建上的最优性差异。

Original Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，用于动力系统零样本重建的基础模型（FMs）在域外泛化方面表现强劲，但对其预测背后的机制却知之甚少。理解这些机制有助于将臃肿的FM架构精简至其核心本质，并揭示动力系统领域内上下文学习所需的最小必要条件。为此，本文迭代地简化了一个近期用于动力系统重建的强SOTA模型DynaMix（Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025），将其化为一个最小可解释的两参数形式，命名为DynaBase。DynaBase通过当前潜在状态与最近上下文邻居及其时序后继的线性混合来生成预测。令人惊讶的是，尽管结构极其简单，DynaBase在混沌和周期系统的零样本动力系统重建中仍展现出高度竞争力，且参数负载极低，比其它基础模型低多个数量级。更为重要的是，这种极致的简洁性允许对动力系统重建指标进行直接的模型优化，以及对预测MSE进行闭式单步解析求解。对DynaBase的理论与实证分析进一步导出了一个单参数映射族，其一端恢复为上下文模仿算法（Zhang & Gilpin, 2026），另一端则呈现混沌（有界但发散）行为。我们进一步展示了不同的训练策略如何导致模型在短期预测或动力系统重建上的最优性差异。因此，DynaBase不仅揭示了产生零样本动力系统重建所需的最小机制，还在一个易理解的数学框架内调和了文献中不同的观察结论。

</details>


### [158] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在结果干扰的序列观测数据中的因果推断问题，提出了基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的高效学习方法，并证明了参数估计的一致性和因果量的可信估计，通过合成实验和COVID-19疫苗效果案例分析验证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在序列观测数据中，不同单元的结果之间存在干扰（outcome interference），且存在潜在混杂因子，传统的因果推断方法难以处理这种复杂依赖结构。本文旨在解决这一挑战，提供一种可计算、理论上可保证的因果估计方法。

Method: 将N个单元的二元结果在T个时间步上的演化建模为马尔可夫过程，每个时间步内单元间依赖通过Ising模型刻画，外部场包含处理效应和潜在混杂因子（具有低秩因子结构）。采用最大伪似然估计（MPLE）学习模型参数。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，证明了从学习模型中采样后因果量的可靠估计。合成实验和基于美国县域COVID-19疫苗率对死亡率的真实案例研究验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPLE方法能够有效处理序列观测数据中因结果干扰导致的因果推断问题，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在序列观测设置中存在结果干扰时的因果推断问题。具体地，我们考虑N个单元上的二元结果在T个时间步上呈马尔可夫性的设定。在每个时间步，N个单元的结果通过Ising模型捕获其依赖关系；每个结果还受到一个外部场的影响，该外部场包含了处理效应以及潜在混杂因子的影响。与面板数据文献类似，这些潜在混杂因子被建模为具有低秩因子结构。我们的数据是来自这一高维分布的一个单样本。为了估计感兴趣的因果量，我们提供了一种基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的计算高效方法来学习模型参数。在温和假设下，我们建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，并证明这转化为从学习模型中采样后对感兴趣因果量的可信估计。我们通过合成实验以及一项真实世界案例研究（调查美国各县疫苗率对COVID-19死亡率的因果效应）验证了该方法的效果。

</details>


### [159] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw 是一个面向百万级 Token RL 后训练的架构感知执行栈，通过分离共享提示词梯度、保留模型特定状态和重放短响应分支，在固定 GPU 预算下实现了超长上下文 RL 训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推理系统的上下文长度已接近百万 Token，而 RL 后训练通常仅支持 256K Token 或更少，导致部署时依赖长度泛化。这一差距在 AI Agent 场景尤为严重，因其长轨迹累积大量观察、工具输出和决策历史。

Method: LongStraw 基于 GRPO，采用三项关键技术：1) 共享提示词前向传播不计算 autograd；2) 仅保留后续 Token 所需的模型特定状态；3) 将短响应分支逐一重放，以额外重放时间换取训练图大小的缩减。

Result: 在 8 块 H20 GPU 上，Qwen3.6-27B 完成分组评分和响应反向传播达 210 万位置；组大小从 2 增至 8 仅增加 0.21 GB 峰值显存；压力测试可达 446 万位置。在 32 块 H20 GPU 上，GLM-5.2 全部 78 层完成 210 万 Token 提示词端到端执行。

Conclusion: LongStraw 在固定 GPU 预算下实现了百万级 Token 的 RL 后训练执行能力，验证了架构感知设计对超长上下文训练的有效性，但提示词状态已分离且部分分布式路径尚不完整，仍需后续完善训练正确性。

Original Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

中文摘要翻译: 推理上下文长度与强化学习后训练之间的差距正在扩大：推理系统正接近百万 Token 的上下文长度，而后训练任务通常仍维持在 256K Token 以下，并依赖部署时的长度泛化能力。这一差距对 AI Agent 尤为重要，因为其观察结果、工具输出、文档和先前的决策会沿着长轨迹不断累积。LongStraw 是一个面向固定 GPU 预算下百万 Token 强化学习后训练的架构感知执行栈，以组相对策略优化（GRPO）为实例进行了实现。它在不启用自动求导的情况下评估共享提示词，仅保留后续 Token 所需的模型特定状态，并逐一重放短响应分支，从而以增加重放时间为代价缩小实时训练图。我们针对混合循环与全注意力机制的 Qwen3.6-27B 以及压缩注意力混合专家模型 GLM-5.2 进行了实现。在八块 H20 GPU 上，对于组大小为 2 和 8 的情况，LongStraw 在 210 万个位置上完成了分组 Qwen 评分与响应反向传播；将组大小增加至 8 仅增加 0.21 GB 的峰值显存占用，而单独的压测实验达到了 446 万个位置。在 32 块 H20 GPU 上，我们针对 GLM-5.2 全部 78 层验证了 210 万 Token 提示词的端到端 LongStraw 执行路径。这些实验建立的是执行容量而非完整的训练正确性，因为捕获的提示词状态已被分离，且部分分布式前向传播与梯度组合路径尚不完整。

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多轴max@K，一种基于强化学习的组级目标函数，用于提升扩散式文生图模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖度，从而增加生成图像的多样性和人口统计公平性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的文生图模型对于同一提示词生成的样本往往只覆盖少部分视觉上不同的模式，导致图像多样性不足；对于以人为中心的提示词，还可能反映或放大人口统计偏见。

Method: 提出多轴max@K方法，这是一个基于组的强化学习目标。给定一组样本和每类目标的一个得分，先对每个类别取组内样本的最大得分，再对所有类别的最大值求和。信用分配机制使得某个样本仅在其能提升该类别的组内最大值时才获得正向权重，从而允许多个样本分别贡献于不同类别。

Result: 在合成混合数据和SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素级颜色奖励验证了信用分配机制；在感知外观公平性评估中，多轴max@K在三个自动评估器上对未见过的提示词将公平性评分提升了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K能够有效提升扩散式文生图模型对目标语义模式的覆盖度，改善生成多样性和人口统计公平性，且不牺牲图像质量与文本对齐能力。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型能够合成逼真且与提示词一致的图像，然而，针对同一提示词生成的样本通常只覆盖少数视觉上不同的模式。这限制了图像的多样性，并且对于以人为中心的提示词，可能反映或放大人口统计偏见。我们将该问题形式化为对一组预定义语义模式（称为目标模式覆盖）的覆盖。我们随后提出多轴max@K，一种基于组的强化学习目标，用于改善扩散式T2I模型中的此类覆盖。给定一组样本和每个目标类别的一个得分，多轴max@K首先对每个类别取样本的最大得分，然后对这些类别维度的最大值求和。由此产生的信用分配机制使得一个样本仅当它提升该类别的组内最大值时才获得该类别上的正向权重，从而允许多个样本分别贡献于不同类别。我们首先在合成混合数据上以及在使用确定性像素级颜色奖励的SD3.5-M上验证了该信用分配机制。然后，我们在感知外观公平性上评估了同一目标。在三个自动评估器上对未见过的提示词，多轴max@K相对于基础模型将公平性评分提升了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐。

</details>


### [161] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MseaCL框架，通过引入语义相似性信号缓解多模态对比学习中的假阴性样本问题，在儿科脑MRI和放射报告中实现更鲁棒的语义对齐，显著提升脑肿瘤分子分类性能（AUC提升≥22.6%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习假设批次中非配对样本均为负样本，但在医学场景中样本间常共享高层语义属性，导致大量假阴性样本，损害表示质量。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在对比学习过程中引入放射报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，以缓解语义相似的假阴性样本对表示学习的负面影响。该框架基于儿科3D脑MRI与放射报告数据进行预训练。

Result: 在下游任务中，尤其是儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务上，AUC至少提升22.6%，表明该框架能显著提升多模态表示的鲁棒性与语义对齐能力。

Conclusion: MseaCL通过语义感知的对比学习有效缓解了医学多模态学习中的假阴性问题，在临床应用中展现出更强的表示能力和迁移潜力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态对比学习在跨数据模态对齐表示和提升下游任务方面展现出显著性能，尤其是在医疗健康领域。其核心思想是最小化匹配（正例）数据模态之间的距离，同时最大化不匹配（负例）样本之间的距离。传统的对比学习框架通常假设批次内样本之间存在基于实例的对应关系，将所有非配对样本视为负样本。然而，这一假设在医学场景中往往不成立——不同样本可能共享高层语义属性，从而产生假阴性样本，损害表示质量。本文提出多模态语义感知对比学习（MseaCL），一个基于儿科队列的3D脑磁共振成像与放射报告训练而成的对比学习框架。该框架的核心目标是通过在训练过程中引入放射报告之间的语义相似性作为引导信号，缓解语义相似的假阴性样本带来的影响。实验结果表明，将该框架作为预训练阶段，可以在下游任务中取得显著提升，例如在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务中，受试者工作特征曲线下面积至少提升22.6%，证明了该方法在临床应用中构建更鲁棒、语义对齐的多模态表示的潜力。

</details>


### [162] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新型离线策略评估估计器，用于上下文赌博机场景，该估计器结合了加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性特性，在行为策略设定错误等复杂条件下表现优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法（如普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样）各有局限：普通重要性采样无偏但方差大，加权重要性采样有界但存在偏差。作者希望结合二者的优点，提出一种既具有有界性又保持线性特性的新估计器。

Method: 提出了Kernel-WIS估计器，通过核方法将加权重要性采样的有界性与普通重要性采样的线性特性相结合，仅利用离线数据进行上下文赌博机的策略评估。

Result: 理论分析证明Kernel-WIS是渐近一致的；实验结果表明该方法在多种复杂条件下（包括行为策略设定错误）显著优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样）。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS是一种有效的离线策略评估工具，兼具理论一致性和实际性能优势，特别适合处理行为策略存在设定错误的复杂场景。

Original Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新颖的估计器，用于仅利用离线数据对上下文赌博机进行策略评估。所提出的Kernel-WIS估计器被证明是渐近一致的，并且在经验上优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样），尤其是在行为策略设定错误等复杂条件下。Kernel-WIS的优势源于将普通加权重要性采样的有界性与普通重要性采样的线性特性相结合。

</details>


### [163] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SINDy（稀疏识别非线性动力学）方法及其在工程中的应用教程，包括标准算法、噪声鲁棒变体、约束和参数化扩展，并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个案例展示了其有效性和实用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工程问题中许多现象的控制方程难以完整获知，神经网络等代理模型需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性，而SINDy方法能从较小数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，但它在实际工程问题中的应用尚未被充分记录。

Method: SINDy方法通过在候选非线性项库上进行稀疏回归，从相对较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程。教程从标准SINDy算法出发，逐步扩展到噪声鲁棒的弱形式变体、基于集成的变体、约束形式和参数化形式。

Result: 通过两个详细案例展示了SINDy在工程中的有效应用：(1)无人机的系统辨识，(2)混沌热虹吸换热器的系统辨识，证明SINDy易于实现且足够灵活，能作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法简单易实现且灵活性强，能够作为高级工程应用中有价值的系统辨识工具，适合无先验知识的读者学习和应用。

Original Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

中文摘要翻译: 许多工程问题涉及的控制方程要么特征不明确，要么仅部分已知。神经网络等代理建模技术能够捕捉这些系统的行为，但它们通常需要大量训练数据——这在工程背景下难以获得，并且生成的模型物理可解释性有限。稀疏识别非线性动力学（SINDy）方法通过在候选非线性项库上进行稀疏回归，从相对较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，从而解决了上述两个局限性。尽管SINDy已在典型基准系统上得到广泛验证，但它在实际工程问题中的应用仍较少被记录。本教程介绍了SINDy方法，并逐步构建其主要扩展，从噪声鲁棒的弱形式和基于集成的变体，到约束形式和参数化形式。本文及配套教程（见https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers）分为三个部分：第一部分介绍标准SINDy算法并逐步扩展，邀请无先验知识的读者跟随每一步并将方法应用于自己的问题；其余两部分展示了(1)无人机系统辨识和(2)混沌热虹吸换热器这两个详细案例研究。通过这些示例，我们旨在证明SINDy实现简单且足够灵活，能够作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

</details>


### [164] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于最优治疗推荐时，利用协变量平衡诊断方法检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的有效性。结果显示现有研究存在高偏倚风险或现有指标不足以评估，因此现有结论缺乏统计稳健性，并提出了未来研究方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 离线强化学习在最优治疗推荐领域的应用日益广泛，但存在隐藏混杂变量和模型误设的风险，现有研究缺乏有效的诊断方法来评估这些偏差，导致研究结论的统计稳健性存疑。

Method: 利用协变量平衡诊断方法，检测离线强化学习研究中隐藏混杂/模型误设的存在。

Result: 现有离线强化学习用于治疗推荐的研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有的协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究；无论如何，现有研究结论不能被认定为统计稳健的。

Conclusion: 提出了未来研究方向，旨在获得方法论上更稳健的离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用。

Original Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文探讨了在将离线强化学习应用于推导最优治疗推荐的研究中，利用协变量平衡诊断方法检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的存在。结果表明，要么现有离线强化学习用于治疗推荐的研究存在高偏倚风险，要么现有的协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究。无论如何，现有的离线强化学习研究不能被认定为统计稳健的。结论提出了未来研究方向，旨在获得方法论上更稳健的离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用。

</details>


### [165] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS框架，利用范畴论中的草图（sketch）和切线提升（tangent lift）来修复机器学习中的非组合性问题，将机器学习形式化为余代数不动点搜索问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器学习方法通常依赖标量化的损失函数或向量空间假设，缺乏处理非组合性（即图表无法通过商草图分解）的通用范畴论框架。本文旨在用严格的数学语言刻画并修复这种非组合性缺陷。

Method: 1) 将机器学习问题定义为草图（含交换条件、极限锥、余极限余锥）；2) 定义非组合性为通用分解问题的障碍（Obs）；3) 应用切线函子T进行切线提升，定义LINCS为分解障碍；4) 引入INC函子迭代执行切线提升，形成塔结构；5) 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 证明了在T_INC具有基于集合的类实现并能创建最终载体的条件下，最终INC余代数存在；详细实验评估在深度学习、大语言模型、强化学习等场景中进行，结果由配套论文描述。

Conclusion: LINCS为机器学习中的组合性问题提供了严格的范畴论修复框架，将ML表述为余代数不动点搜索，为理解和改进模型行为提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

中文摘要翻译: 本文开发了一个范畴论框架——无穷小非组合性草图中的学习（LINCS）——作为非组合性的修复：即图表无法通过商草图分解的失败，该框架被提升到切范畴设置中。机器学习问题被规范化为草图：带有交换条件$\mathcal D$、极限锥$\mathcal L$和余极限余锥$\mathcal K$的图，推广了通常的损失函数标量化或向量空间假设。非组合性被纯粹定义为通用分解问题的失败，而非期望预测与实际预测之间的算术误差。给定一个学习草图$\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$（其底层图为$S$）和一个模型$D:J \rightarrow C$，基缺陷是分解$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$的障碍。切线提升应用切线函子$T$得到$TD:J \rightarrow C$，而LINCS被定义为障碍$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$——询问无穷小扰动是否保持组合性约束。本文还引入了切线学习草图，即配备了Cockett-Cruttwell切线结构的草图。本文定义了INC自函子，它迭代执行切线提升，产生一个分解问题的塔$D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$。机器学习因此被表述为对余代数不动点的搜索，其中连续的切线展开趋于稳定（$νT_{\mbox{INC}}$）。利用Aczel-Mendler定理，我们证明了只要$T_{\mbox{INC}}$允许一个基于集合的类实现并创建其最终载体，最终INC余代数就存在。LINCS在包括深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习在内的多个具体机器学习场景中的详细实验评估正在进行中，并在配套论文中加以描述。

</details>


### [166] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)的新型蒸馏方法，通过引入delta信号（教师模型与基础推理模型之间的差异）替代直接模仿教师输出分布，显著提升了在线策略蒸馏在数学、科学和代码推理任务上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但忽略了教师模型因推理调优而产生的关键变化，缺乏更直接的推理能力迁移信号。

Method: 定义delta信号为教师模型与其在推理指令微调之前的基础模型之间的差异，使用该差值作为蒸馏奖励信号替代直接模仿教师输出分布，提出On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上，OPD² consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation，使推理LLM在仅需短时间后训练的情况下即可获得强性能。

Conclusion: delta信号能够更直接地捕获推理调优带来的变化，为推理能力迁移提供更有效的监督信号，从而大幅提升在线策略蒸馏的效果。

Original Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

中文摘要翻译: 在线策略蒸馏是强化学习中一种替代性的后训练方法，通过从教师模型提供token级别的监督来缓解奖励模型带来的约束。尽管在线策略蒸馏已在多种设置下得到研究和应用，但其基本设计仍未得到充分探索。在本文中，我们引入了一种新的蒸馏奖励，称为delta信号，以替代直接模仿教师输出分布。delta信号被定义为教师模型与其在进行推理能力指令微调之前的基础模型之间的差异。因此，它捕获了推理调优所引起的变化，并为迁移推理能力提供了更直接的信号。通过大量实证证据，我们证明了delta信号显著提升了在线策略蒸馏的效果，并将这种新的蒸馏方法称为在线策略delta蒸馏（OPD²）。在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的实验表明，OPD² consistently优于传统的在线策略蒸馏，使推理大语言模型仅需短时间的后训练即可实现强性能。代码将发布在https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2。

</details>


### [167] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，在保留已知ODE结构的同时用神经网络学习未知动态，通过RTS平滑器与反向传播交替迭代，从部分观测中重建潜状态并预测长期轨迹。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多实际系统中部分动力学方程未知且仅能观测到部分状态变量，传统纯数据驱动方法缺乏可解释性，而纯物理模型无法处理未知成分，因此需要一种既能保留已知可解释结构又能学习未知动态的混合方法。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代方法：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，使用Rauch-Tung-Striebel (RTS)平滑器从观测数据中推断潜状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。重复迭代直至满足预设准则。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上评估，所提方法能在部分观测条件下学习未知ODE组件，同时利用并保留可解释的机制结构，改善了潜状态重建和长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 该混合框架有效结合了物理建模的可解释性与神经网络的灵活性，能够在部分观测和部分动力学未知的场景下准确学习系统动态，提升状态估计和长期预测能力。

Original Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 常微分方程广泛应用于物理、生物学、神经科学和生理学中的动力系统建模，但在许多应用中，部分动力学方程是未知的，且仅有部分状态变量可被观测。我们提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，其中已知的ODE分量保持显式表达，缺失分量由神经网络表示。该方法包含两个阶段，交替进行状态估计与参数估计，并迭代直至满足预设准则。具体而言，第一步将模型参数视为已知，使用Rauch-Tung-Striebel (RTS)平滑器从可用观测中推断潜状态；第二步将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。我们在涵盖线性、非线性和刚性动力学的基准系统上进行了评估，这些系统均处于部分状态观测条件下。实验结果表明，所提方法能够从不完整的观测中学习缺失的ODE分量，同时利用并保留可解释的机制结构，改善了潜状态重建和长期预测性能。

</details>


### [168] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，系统研究世界-动作模型（WAM）中的世界-动作漂移攻击，发现通过微小视觉扰动可破坏WAM中动作生成与未来世界预测之间的对齐，导致任务失败。攻击分为动作破坏型和想象保持型两种，实验表明攻击能显著降低任务成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）被视为具身控制的有前途范式，其核心优势在于动作生成与未来世界预测的耦合被认为能带来鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性。本文旨在质疑这一假设，探究这种耦合是否真的可靠，并系统研究针对WAM的对抗性攻击。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义世界-动作漂移攻击这一新攻击类别。沿两个标准（攻击强度和隐蔽性）设计两类攻击：(1) 动作破坏型攻击，直接驱使模型执行导致任务失败的动作；(2) 想象保持型攻击，在保持模型预测的未来接近干净想象的同时诱导有害动作偏移。两类攻击共同覆盖从显式动作劫持到隐蔽动作漂移的WAM特有故障谱系。

Result: 在多种WAM变体上评估，动作破坏型攻击将任务成功率从96.5%降至43.1%；想象保持型攻击揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化能在保持强攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移。

Conclusion: WAM中动作生成与未来预测的耦合并非天然鲁棒，存在可被利用的安全漏洞。BadWAM框架暴露了WAM特有的攻击面，对未来WAM的安全性设计提出了警示。

Original Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

中文摘要翻译: 世界-动作模型（World-action models, WAMs）正成为具身控制的一种有前景的基础范式：它们不仅仅预测动作，而是学习将动作生成与未来世界预测耦合起来的表示。这种耦合通常被视为鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性的来源，因为原则上可以对照机器人想象出的未来来检查其动作。在本文中，我们展示了这一假设是脆弱的。我们提出了BadWAM，一个用于建模和评估世界-动作漂移攻击（World-Action Drift Attacks）的统一框架：这是一类新的WAM特有的对抗性攻击，通过微小的视觉扰动破坏WAM所想象的内容与其实际执行动作之间的对齐。BadWAM沿两个自然标准——攻击强度和隐蔽性——刻画了这一攻击面。当攻击者优先考虑破坏性时，BadWAM实例化为动作破坏型对抗性攻击，直接驱使模型执行导致任务失败的动作。当攻击者额外优先考虑隐蔽性时，BadWAM实例化为想象保持型对抗性攻击，旨在诱导有害的动作偏移，同时保持模型预测的未来接近其干净的想象。这两种攻击共同涵盖了WAM特有的故障谱系：从显式的动作劫持到更隐蔽的情况，即模型看似想象了一个合理的未来，但执行了与之不同步的动作。我们在不同WAM变体上评估了BadWAM。结果表明，在闭环执行下，我们的攻击显著降低了任务成功率。例如，动作破坏型攻击将模型性能从96.5%的成功率降至43.1%。想象保持型攻击的结果进一步暴露了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化可以在保持强攻击性能的同时减少未来想象的漂移。

</details>


### [169] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多台独立同质设备的块替换策略维护问题，提出数据驱动的多臂老虎机算法，在不了解寿命分布的情况下学习成本最优替换间隔，并实现了与理论下界匹配的遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 设备维护中的块替换策略需要确定最优替换间隔，但实际中设备寿命分布未知，需要从运行数据中自适应学习，传统方法依赖分布假设，缺乏理论保障。

Method: 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein的置信下界算法；利用块替换特有的嵌套观测性质设计改进算法；提出Kaplan-Meier更新算法非参数估计寿命分布；并建立两个平均成本MDP模型进行分析。

Result: 标准算法实现O(K log T)遗憾匹配Lai-Robbins下界；相关变体实现O((K-k*)log T)遗憾且对次优臂仅需O(1)次直接拉动；Kaplan-Meier算法实现几乎必然一致性；MDP分析证明块替换在其策略类中的最优性及单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动算法在理论上和实证上都能有效学习最优块替换间隔，且揭示了块替换与年龄依赖替换之间的结构性成本差距。

Original Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

中文摘要翻译: 我们开发了用于维护$N$台独立同质设备的数据驱动算法，采用\textit{块替换策略}：每台设备故障时立即替换，同时所有设备每隔$k$个时间单位集体替换一次。目标是在寿命分布未知的情况下，从运行数据中学习成本最小的替换间隔$k^*$。在每个决策时刻，操作者选择$k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$，观察由此产生的故障历史（包含完全寿命和右删失寿命的混合数据），并承担由更新函数决定的单位时间成本。我们将此建模为随机多臂老虎机问题，提出了基于Hoeffding和Bernstein的置信下界算法，实现了$O(K \log T)$的遗憾界，匹配Lai-Robbins下界。利用块替换特有的嵌套观测性质，相关变体算法实现了$O((K-k^*)\log T)$的遗憾，并且对次优臂$k < k^*$仅需$O(1)$次直接拉动。补充的Kaplan-Meier更新算法从删失数据中非参数地估计寿命分布，实现了几乎必然的策略一致性，并在长期视野下达到经验上的近零增量遗憾。我们还分析了两个平均成本马尔可夫决策过程：时间流逝模型证明了块替换在其策略类中对任意寿命分布的最优性；年龄向量模型在递增失效率分布下证明了单调阈值结构，并提供了黄金标准的成本基准。数值实验验证了理论排序，并揭示了最优块替换与年龄依赖替换之间的结构性成本差距。

</details>


### [170] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，使用KP-Tree在用户评分到达时即时更新嵌入，解决两阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题，无需完整模型重训练即可实现快速个性化推荐。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 两阶段推荐系统中存在嵌入陈旧问题：用户对项目评分后，其嵌入直到下一次重训练周期才更新，导致推荐性能受限。

Method: 将用户偏好存储在KP-Tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）中，仅需拟合一次低秩投影，当评分到达时动态在线重计算嵌入。

Result: 在KuaiRec上，可变草图仅读取1.8%数据即达到0.810 RMSE，优于ALS读取100%数据时的0.822 RMSE；每批更新速度快8倍；新用户首次评分后可在1毫秒内获得个性化推荐；在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上，KP-Tree的范数比例采样比均匀采样提供40-130%更好的项目覆盖率。

Conclusion: 可变草图能有效消除嵌入陈旧问题，在显著降低数据读取和计算成本的同时，实现优于传统方法的推荐精度，并支持即时个性化推荐。

Original Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

中文摘要翻译: 两阶段推荐中的一个常见瓶颈是嵌入陈旧问题：当用户对新的项目进行评分时，其嵌入向量在下一次重训练周期之前保持不变。我们提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches），该方法将每个用户的偏好存储在一个KP-Tree（一种带有求和聚合的稀疏线段树）中，仅拟合一次低秩投影，并在评分到达时动态地在线重计算嵌入。我们证明每个新观测值都会单调地收紧预测误差包络（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS所缺乏的保证。在KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图仅读取1.8%的数据即可达到0.810的RMSE，而ALS在读取100%数据时为0.822，且每批更新速度快8倍。新用户在完成首次评分后，无需任何模型重训练，即可在1毫秒内获得个性化推荐。对不同密度场景下采样策略的比较表明，KP-Tree的范数比例采样在稀疏数据（密度<1%）上提供比均匀采样高出40-130%的项目覆盖率，而在稠密矩阵上均匀采样已足够。

</details>


### [171] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文的新方法，使用机器学习（XGBoost）分类比特币市场情绪，F1-score达0.84，并通过SHAP解释特征贡献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的比特币研究多聚焦于价格预测，而本文旨在解释市场情绪，通过融合链上交易数据、历史价格数据和Twitter情绪分类，理解比特币市场行为背后的情绪驱动因素。

Method: 将社交媒体情绪趋势与链上指标及金融指标融合，归一化形成数据集；采用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证，其中XGBoost表现最佳；使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析，量化链上特征对分类结果的贡献。

Result: XGBoost在情绪分类任务中平均F1-score达到约0.84，为最可靠模型；SHAP分析表明链上特征对模型预测有显著贡献，证明了数据组合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该数据组合方法能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来结合深度学习进行改进提供了基础。

Original Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 随着比特币作为一种去中心化数字资产和投资工具的日益普及，人们对其市场行为的理解产生了浓厚兴趣。本研究提出了一种新方法，通过结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体帖子来分析比特币市场情绪。与旨在预测价格的模型不同，本文专注于利用区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类来解释市场情绪。该方法将情绪趋势与链上指标和金融指标融合，经归一化处理后形成数据集，用于详细的市场分析。通过交叉验证测试了多种机器学习模型，其中梯度提升（XGBoost）在情绪分类中表现最为可靠，平均F1分数达到约0.84。SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）是一种基于博弈论的模型可解释性方法，被用于量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献，提高了模型透明度。结果表明，这种数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，为数据驱动的加密货币分析提供了支持，并为未来结合深度学习进行改进奠定了基础。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Tight Wavelet Frames on Graphs via Isometric Group Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14168)
*Rigobert Fokam Souop,Laurent Bitjoka*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图在有限阿贝尔群Cayley图中等距嵌入的图谱小波构造新框架。通过将图嵌入到可进行经典傅里叶分析的宿主群中，构造宿主上的小波再限制回原图。两种构造：膨胀小波（需宿主有复合循环因子）和谱带通小波（通用，构成Parseval紧框架，可精确重构，平移协变，顶点-频率联合局部化）。通过离散调和延拓完成信号补全，变换复杂度O(JN log N)。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图谱小波在不规则图上存在非规范特征基、非紧框架、需反转框架算子重构等问题，缺乏精确的数学基底。本文旨在建立一种基于精确代数基底的图小波构造方法，克服上述缺陷。

Method: 1) 将连通图等距嵌入到有限阿贝尔群Cayley图（宿主）中；2) 在宿主上构造小波（两种方案：膨胀小波利用群自同构实现膨胀，谱带通小波利用归一化滤波器组）；3) 将宿主小波限制回原图；4) 通过离散调和延拓将图信号完备地扩展到宿主剩余节点。

Result: 谱带通小波构成Parseval紧框架，满足恒等式，可精确重构，平移协变；变换复杂度O(JN log N)；在基准宿主上重构达到机器精度；带通原子89-99%的能量集中在距中心图距离2的范围内。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了图小波分析的精确代数基础，克服了传统图谱小波的紧框架缺失和重构复杂问题，具有良好的计算效率和局部化性能。

Original Abstract: Spectral graph wavelets apply a kernel to the graph Laplacian spectrum. On an irregular graph their analyzing functions inherit a non-canonical eigenbasis, they do not form a tight frame, and reconstruction requires inverting a frame operator. We take a different route, built on an exact substrate. Given an isometric embedding of a connected graph into a Cayley graph of a finite abelian group, a host on which classical Fourier analysis applies exactly, we construct wavelets on the host and restrict them to the graph. Two constructions arise and we keep them separate. Dilation wavelets use a group automorphism as a dilation, reproducing the classical translate-dilate template but existing only on hosts with composite cyclic factors. Spectral band-pass wavelets use a normalized filter bank in the dual frequency magnitude; they exist on every host, form a Parseval (tight) frame, reconstruct any graph signal exactly via restriction, are translation-covariant, and localize jointly in vertex and frequency. We prove the tight-frame identity and exact reconstruction, give a multiresolution decomposition, and show the full transform costs O(JN log N) via the host fast Fourier transform. For a proper embedding we show the canonical way to complete a signal onto the host remainder is the discrete harmonic extension, which uniquely minimizes the host Dirichlet energy and places the zero-padding and symmetric-extension heuristics as approximations of it. On benchmark hosts reconstruction reaches machine precision and band-pass atoms concentrate 89-99 percent of their energy within graph-distance two of their center.

中文摘要翻译: 谱图小波对图拉普拉斯谱应用一个核函数。在不规则图上，其分析函数继承非规范特征基，不构成紧框架，重构需要反转框架算子。我们采取了不同的路径，构建在精确的代数基底之上。给定一个连通图到有限阿贝尔群Cayley图的等距嵌入——一个经典傅里叶分析可精确适用的宿主——我们在宿主上构造小波并将其限制回原图。产生两种构造并保持分离。膨胀小波使用群自同构作为膨胀，重现经典的平移-膨胀模板，但仅存在于具有复合循环因子的宿主上。谱带通小波在频域幅度中使用归一化滤波器组；它们存在于每个宿主上，构成Parseval（紧）框架，通过限制精确重构任何图信号，具有平移协变性，并在顶点和频率上联合局部化。我们证明了紧框架恒等式和精确重构，给出了多分辨率分解，并展示了通过宿主快速傅里叶变换，完整变换的计算复杂度为O(JN log N)。对于恰当的嵌入，我们展示了将信号规范地补全到宿主剩余部分的方法是离散调和延拓，它唯一地最小化宿主狄利克雷能量，并将零填充和对称扩展启发式方法视为其近似。在基准宿主上，重构达到机器精度，带通原子将其89-99%的能量集中在距中心图距离二的范围内。

</details>


### [173] [Denoising-Autoencoder-Assisted Physical Layer Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14505)
*Zhengyu Xu,Shun Kojima,Shinya Sugiura*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于去噪自编码器（DAE）的密钥生成方案，通过抑制信道测量中的噪声非互易性，显著提升了密钥生成的可靠性与效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无线信道测量中存在的信道噪声非互易性问题会降低密钥生成的一致性和效率，现有方法对此抑制不足。

Method: 利用去噪自编码器（DAE）捕获输入分布的潜在结构，在保留信号本质结构的同时去除加性噪声，从而实现信道测量噪声的抑制。

Result: 所提方案在密钥不一致率（KDR）、密钥容量（SKC）以及生成密钥的随机性方面均优于传统方案。

Conclusion: DAE辅助的密钥生成方案能够有效提升无线信道密钥生成的性能和安全性。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose denoising autoencoder (DAE)-assisted secret key generation (SKG), where channel noise reciprocity imperfections induced due to wireless channel measurements are suppressed, hence significantly enhancing the reliability and efficiency. More specifically, the DAE is capable of capturing the intrinsic structure of input distributions, reconstructing the original data structure, and removing additive noise while preserving the essential structure of signals. In our analysis, it is demonstrated that the proposed SKG scheme exhibits higher performance than the conventional schemes in terms of key disagreement rate (KDR), secret key capacity (SKC), and randomness of the generated keys.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种去噪自编码器（DAE）辅助的密钥生成方案，该方案能够抑制由无线信道测量引起的信道噪声非互易性，从而显著提升密钥生成的可靠性与效率。具体而言，DAE能够捕获输入分布的潜在结构，重构原始数据结构，并在保留信号本质结构的同时去除加性噪声。分析表明，所提出的密钥生成方案在密钥不一致率（KDR）、密钥容量（SKC）以及生成密钥的随机性方面均优于传统方案。

</details>


### [174] [SLIPT-Enabled Ground-to-UAV FSO Systems with Optical Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14523)
*Luna Sugiyama,Phuc V. Trinh,Shinya Sugiura*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光学可重构智能表面（ORIS）辅助的地面到无人机自由空间光通信系统，结合同时光信息与功率传输（SLIPT）技术，以克服视距限制，提高能量收集效率，并降低中断概率和符号错误率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统FSO-based SLIPT系统受限于视距（LoS）传输要求，当无人机处于非视距（non-LoS）位置时，系统性能严重下降。为了克服这一限制，引入ORIS将激光束反射至非视距无人机接收端，从而扩展系统应用场景。

Method: 建立包含大气损耗、湍流引起的衰落、指向误差以及无人机悬停导致的到达角波动的综合信道模型，推导出能量收集、中断概率和符号错误率的闭式表达式，并进行数值分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，集成ORIS能够显著提高能量收集效率，同时中断概率和符号错误率保持在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: ORIS辅助的SLIPT-FSO系统能有效克服视距限制，在保证通信质量的同时提升能量收集性能，为无人机无线光通信与能量传输提供了一种可行方案。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes an optical reconfigurable intelligent surface (ORIS)-assisted ground-to-unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) free-space optical (FSO) communication system empowered by simultaneous lightwave information and power transfer (SLIPT). To overcome the line-of-sight (LoS) limitation of FSO-based SLIPT systems, we introduce an ORIS that reflects the laser beam towards a non-LoS UAV receiver. We model and analyze the combined channel characteristics, incorporating atmospheric loss, turbulence-induced fading, pointing error, and angle-of-arrival fluctuations due to UAV hovering. We derive closed-form expressions for harvested energy, outage probability, and symbol error rate (SER). Numerical results show that integrating ORIS improves EH efficiency while maintaining manageable outage and SER performance.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种光学可重构智能表面（ORIS）辅助的地面到无人机（UAV）自由空间光（FSO）通信系统，该系统采用同时光信息与功率传输（SLIPT）技术。为克服基于FSO的SLIPT系统在视距（LoS）传输上的局限性，我们引入ORIS将激光束反射至非视距（non-LoS）的无人机接收端。我们对包含大气损耗、湍流引起的衰落、指向误差以及无人机悬停导致的到达角波动在内的综合信道特性进行了建模与分析，并推导出了能量收集、中断概率和符号错误率（SER）的闭式表达式。数值结果表明，集成ORIS能在保持可接受的中断和SER性能的同时，提高能量收集效率。

</details>


### [175] [Adaptive Score-Based VAMP: Self-Tuning Hyperparameters via Tilted EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14597)
*Siqi Na,Tadashi Wadayama*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应SC-VAMP方法，通过局部倾斜EM更新模块参数，在标准状态演化假设下可实现贝叶斯最优固定点，数值实验证明了其对强失配初始化的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
